# PA11 port ledger

## Full-stage acceptance audit: nested anonymous initialization

Audit entry HEAD: `a55eac5` (2026-10-15).
Last audited implementation commit: `9f86a5da4cb59a98587a8233b2d5ef47f1be8346`.
Stage base: `24153befb40f2636a35c7cf03ba31de32a575200`.
This ledger-only checkpoint records the reviewed implementation identity;
compiler sources, fixtures and validation outputs are unchanged. Target **pa11
full-stage**, phase **audit**. The preceding turn was **progress**: `8c73ff7`
and `a55eac5` repaired injected-member access/storage and private anonymous base
tail reuse, while preserving the two unfinished initialization reproducers.
This increment closes both original reproducers. **PA11 is not accepted:**
follow-up source review/probes identify four anonymous object/typedef naming
failures below. Ralph retains its existing goal and stage advancement.

### Completed behavior and findings

Frozen C++ `member_plans`, `member_plan` and `mem_initializer_target` keep an
anonymous struct/union's actions inside the enclosing constructor. A written
initializer designates its leaf and each containing anonymous object. A union
counts direct variant objects: initializing two fields of one anonymous struct
selects one variant, while selecting another variant is rejected. Explicit
initializers suppress the corresponding default initializer, including unused
union variants, and execution follows member declaration order.

Rust now records those nested actions as typed constructor plans containing
original member/initializer identities. Each written enclosing constructor
owns its selected anonymous subplans, so overloads with different initializers
do not share selections. A query-local designation memo retains both found
and absent results; an explicit worklist resolves each anonymous member once
within that immutable written-initializer context. Default plans reuse the
existing completion/demand service. Anonymous plans execute inline and never
demand an anonymous constructor callable merely to initialize their members.

`lower/member_initialization.rs` consumes the common plan view for default and
written constructors. It retains the enclosing `this`, computes typed storage
origins for scalar/reference/array/class members, and preserves the distinct
anonymous bit-field projection form. Entering an anonymous group resets its
bit run and restores the enclosing run afterwards. Materialization and aggregate
initialization use that same offset service. Named subobject cleanup registration
retains the frozen anonymous grouping boundary; no whole-body rewrite or emitted
text inspection is involved. Throw analysis visits the inline actions directly.

Further probes exposed and repaired three associated gaps:

- Nontrivial class subobjects inside an anonymous struct require its implicit
  destructor callable. Its source name is absent; Rust now preserves that fact
  separately from its owning scope. Display labels use the scope's recorded
  token span, while the ABI uses a typed `UnnamedType` ordinal. One declaration
  walk numbers each visible class's unnamed member types, including unused
  anonymous classes/enums and excluding typedef-named ones. Private member types
  retain their unit's mangling-order identities. Alias order and ordinary,
  reversed and shifted two-unit outputs match.
- The course driver prepends the frozen hosted builtin declaration vocabulary
  before user input. Thus its anonymous labels include that token origin. The
  driver now derives the origin by lexing that exact vocabulary once into an
  immutable count, then seeds the syntax unit's provenance. This is a lexical
  fact, not a calibrated numeric offset or a second parse of user input. Source
  locations, the streaming window and bounded parser checkpoints are unchanged.
  The hosted declarations' type/template semantics remain later-stage work.
- User-provided bodies do not check an anonymous member's destructor as a named
  subobject's, and an implicit union destructor destroys no variant. Rust now
  follows both frozen rules, allowing an enclosing user destructor to destroy
  its selected union member explicitly. Conversely, a deleted implicit anonymous
  default must propagate as a typed deletion fact to the enclosing constructor:
  rejecting early at offset zero gave incorrect semantic diagnostics for
  reference/const members, nontrivial union defaults and deleted/private
  destructors. All five retained normal rejection positions now match at the
  enclosing object's use. These semantic errors receive no stack exception.

Read the governing contract/specification, conversion plan, full PA11 handout,
current ledger and source map; consulted frozen implementation commit
`3438f5aea24d8fcc9a0113316f7e27c890cd5ac6` directly.

| Reviewed C++ responsibilities | Rust owners / resulting contract |
| --- | --- |
| `sema/analyzer_construction.cpp::member_plans/member_plan/plan_designated/mem_initializer_target` | `sema/member_construction.rs`, `construction.rs`: source-ordered nested plans, direct union designations, initializer precedence, duplicate/access/failure order and original syntax IDs |
| `implicit_default_facts/anonymous_default_facts/inner_plan_facts` | `construction.rs`, `constructor_initialization.rs`: completion memo, inline demand exclusion, selected default/nontrivial/nothrow/deleted facts; anonymous deletion propagates to its enclosing use |
| `check_subobject_destruction` and `lower/object_lowering.cpp::needs_destruction` | `sema/destruction.rs`, `lower/symbols.rs`, `sema/lowering.rs`: anonymous-member checks and implicit-union runtime destruction exclusion, while ordinary named subobject/readonly properties remain distinct |
| `lower/object_lowering.cpp::initialize_member/initialize_scalar_member/this_holder_offset` | `lower/member_initialization.rs`, `construction.rs`, `aggregates.rs`, `materialization.rs`, `throw_scan.rs`: inline actions in one function frame, exact origins/projections/run boundaries, selected recursive construction and existing cleanup policy |
| `lower/symbol_lowering.cpp::unnamed_ordinal/type_unqualified_name`, `local_mangling.cpp::visible_type/anonymous_name` | `anonymous_names.rs`, `symbols.rs`, `sema/anonymous_types.rs`, `local_classes.rs`: canonical IDs, declaration-order visible member ordinals, private member numbering and optional destructor spelling; standalone/typedef gaps explicitly remain below |
| `abi/itanium/abi_graph.h/.cpp` and `abi_encoder.cpp` unnamed-type terminal | `abi/graph.rs`, `encoder.rs`: interned `UnnamedType(u32)` emits `Ut_` / `Ut(number-1)_` directly through the existing encoder |
| `driver/host_environment.cpp::kBuiltinDeclarations`, driver setup and parse-token provenance | `driver/host_declarations.rs`, `ast.rs`, `syntax/mod.rs`, `cursor.rs`: fixed vocabulary produces a typed LowIR token origin; no user-token replay or environment read |

Published plans own compact IDs and initializer views of the original syntax,
not copied trees. Each enclosing constructor's written context bounds its memo;
no incomplete cross-context cache key or global invalidation was introduced.
Private typed stores publish checked IDs. Anonymous ABI caches live with the
unit and never use presentation strings for semantic equality. Only an immutable
fixed-vocabulary count persists process-wide. Function builders and backend
lifetimes are unchanged. Target offsets retain explicit wrapping arithmetic;
host IDs/counts use checked conversions. New recursion uses the existing byte
budget guard, with no cutoff or frame-size calibration.

The added modules own member action planning, member action emission, anonymous
ABI names, and driver vocabulary provenance. They are not numbered/mechanical
splits. There is no unsafe/FFI code, dependency, C++ production fallback,
fixture recognition, embedded reference output, lint allowance, environment
read under `dev/src`, optimizer policy change or register allocator change.
All due frontend work runs in Rust. The source map records the specific owners
and still-open anonymous object naming responsibilities.

| `object-names/typedef-nested.cpp` | 0 / 0, different bytes | Same declaration-time destructor spelling with nested anonymous storage |
| `object-names/local-object.cpp` | 86 / 0: `anonymous constructor name` | A standalone anonymous local class needs its own constructor entry and frozen local display/ABI identity; inline member plans are not that entry |
| `object-names/namespace-object.cpp` | 86 / 0: `anonymous constructor name` | Standalone namespace anonymous object construction and callable identity |

`object_names.py`, `object-names.log`, `object-names.jsonl`, generated sources
and the receipt-linked mismatch directories preserve the exact evidence. These

## Full-stage acceptance audit: layout and injected member storage

Audit entry HEAD: `2e21cfd` (2026-10-15).
Last audited implementation commit: `8c73ff70ea9772e45d3526a8db502bfde4781c0a`.
Stage base: `24153befb40f2636a35c7cf03ba31de32a575200`.
This ledger-only checkpoint records the reviewed implementation identity;
compiler sources, fixtures and validation outputs are unchanged. Target
**pa11 full-stage**, phase **audit**. **PA11 is not accepted:** two independently
reproduced anonymous constructor-initialization paths still return status 86.
Passing fixtures do not discharge these responsibilities. Ralph's existing goal
remains active; no goal, state, acceptance policy or frozen reference was changed.

### Completed findings

The direct source comparison exposed three related injected-member defects and
a separate layout fact omitted at anonymous storage declaration:

- Implicit member lookup tested the original anonymous class against `this`.
  It rejected valid uses of an injected member even inside its enclosing class.
  `anonymous_types.rs` now owns a typed holder query over the original member's
  owned storage identities, stopping at the class being named. Member selection
  retains the resulting enclosing base path; a zero-offset base projection is
  still emitted when frozen C++ emits it.
- Scalar access omitted the byte offsets of enclosing anonymous objects and
  chose union memory facts from the innermost declaration. Lowering now sums
  their offsets and checks the actual named holder/object classes, reproducing
  the exact field projections and typed/untyped loads and stores. Bit-field
  access uses the same naming boundary while retaining its local unit layout.
- Namespace pointer/reference initializers likewise used only the leaf offset.
  Constant place evaluation now completes the naming-class layout and consumes
  its recorded base path and anonymous storage offsets. Array indexing composes
  with that address. No later phase reconstructs lookup from text.
- A private/protected anonymous storage declaration did not publish its
  enclosing class's non-POD layout category. In the retained `private-storage`
  reproducer Rust consequently emitted a 12-byte derived object and placed its
  last member at offset 8; frozen C++ emits 8 bytes with that member at offset 5.
  The declaration now publishes the missing typed fact, preserving tail reuse.
  It deliberately leaves aggregate eligibility unchanged, as the frozen
  `anonymous_union` path does; ordinary named data-member rules are separate.

The new executed PA11 control
`tests/controls/anonymous-inherited-member-access.t` checks direct and inherited
injected names, explicit qualified access, implicit `this`, nested nonzero
storage offsets, signed bit fields and adjacent fields, volatile access,
namespace pointer/reference/array relocations, and private anonymous base tail
reuse. Its independent exact LowIR comparison supplements its runtime outcome.
No previous fixture was edited or formatted.

### Source responsibilities and architecture review

Read the governing contract/specification, conversion plan, complete PA11
handout, current ledger and source map. Consulted frozen C++ directly, including
its nonvirtual layout, class declaration/completion, access, expression,
constant-place and member-lowering owners. The frozen reference remains commit
`3438f5aea24d8fcc9a0113316f7e27c890cd5ac6`.

| `layout.cpp::begin/end_bit_run/assign_bit_units/assign_bit_segment/assign_bit_fields/NarrowStorageWindow` | `bit_layout.rs`, `bit_fields.rs`: packed versus natural units, unnamed/zero/wide fields, signed and enum identity, run partitioning, narrow windows, split/shared units and final open limit compared |
| `layout.cpp::base_conflict/member_conflict/empty_subobjects/spend_layout_work` | `empty_layout.rs`: same empty-class/address identities, traversal order, array stepping, record limit, conflict hint and bounded work; owned explicit worklists replace recursion |
| `analyzer_classes.cpp::new_class/class_specifier/class_body/anonymous_union/elaborated_class/finish_class_nesting` | `declarations.rs`, `anonymous_types.rs`, `local_classes.rs`, `data_members.rs`, `layout_attributes.rs`: original declaration ownership and complete-class deferred phases reviewed; injected identity and missing private-storage layout fact repaired; anonymous initialization remains open below |
| `analyzer_access.cpp::declare_in/member_or_friend_of/access_chain/accessible_below/check_access/check_function_access/check_special_access/begin/defer/settle_declaration_access` | `graph.rs`, `member_expressions.rs`, `inheritance.rs`, `friendships.rs`, `declaration_access.rs`: lexical/naming/object identities, single-base accessibility, protected-object checks, post-resolution function access and declaration settlement compared; template/virtual/multiple-base responsibilities are later boundaries |
| `analyzer_expressions.cpp::member_of_object` and `lower/program_lowering.cpp::member_holder/anonymous_home` | `member_expressions.rs`, `scalar_expressions.rs`, `anonymous_types.rs`, `lowering.rs`: cv/category/reference/bit-field identity retained; implicit-object and holder/base-route gaps repaired |
| `lower/expression_lowering.cpp::member_place` and `aggregate_lowering.cpp` bit-field place | `mutations.rs`, `base_projections.rs`, `field_updates.rs`: exact projection order, holder-relative offset and union memory facts repaired |
| `sema/constant_interpreter.cpp::member_slot/member_place` | `constant_addresses.rs`: static field address now includes typed base and anonymous projections; pointer/reference/array static outputs match |

The new service follows owned `EntityId`/`BindingId` edges; it does not create
another syntax/semantic tree, store a pointer graph, synthesize name keys or
repeat name lookup. Existing `BasePathId` facts remain the conversion authority.
Layout completion and selected demand remain explicit; no cache key or
invalidation policy changes. Anonymous offset addition wraps explicitly in the
target `u64` domain, and constant addresses retain signed target interpretation.
No parser transaction, backend policy or optimizer/register allocator changes;
function-owned lowering state still dies at the existing streaming boundary.
No environment read, process implementation fallback, unsafe/FFI code,
dependency, blanket allowance, fixture recognition or reference answer was added.
The source map records the specific translated responsibilities and open paths.

## Full-stage acceptance audit: persistent declaration-time temporaries

The following ledger-only commit records this identity; validation and compiler
sources are unchanged.
Stage base: `24153befb40f2636a35c7cf03ba31de32a575200`. Target **pa11
full-stage**, phase **audit**. The previous goal turn was **progress**: commits
`ca8189c` and `bda99a6` repaired stored-object cleanup and recorded independent
remaining reproducers. This increment resolves the namespace declaration-time
persistent lifetime group. **Full-stage acceptance remains unproven pending the
remaining PA11 responsibility audit.** Ralph retains acceptance and advancement.

### Scope correction and completed findings

The earlier audit classified block-static class storage as a PA11 prerequisite.
Direct review of `pa16/README.md`'s Assignment Boundary and the introductory
contract in frozen `lower/local_static_lowering.cpp` shows it is **PA16-owned**:
function-local dynamic class statics and static reference temporaries, their
first-entry guards and exit registration. The retained
`before-boundaries/block-static-class.cpp` status-86 receipt remains an accurate
later-stage port boundary. It does not prevent PA11 acceptance. This corrects
the preceding ledger's classification without changing a handout or gate.

The other two retained status-86 reproducers are supported namespace
reference/aggregate declaration paths. They now produce the exact C++ LowIR:
unique static storage, construction-completion flags, and finalizers placed in
reverse construction order. A class/array/converting temporary becomes a
persistent finalizer only after materialization completes; it leaves no
function-owned cleanup. Reference arrays retain C++'s distinct small-array
address/constant-offset form and the existing loop beyond eight elements.
An enclosing aggregate's destructor precedes its reference temporaries.
Conditional branches destroy only completed objects. Subobject/base/comma
bindings preserve the original reference-extension path.

Source review also distinguishes thread-local initialization from namespace
persistence. The frozen C++ course path keeps a thread reference's binding in
its frame without destroying it there; a TLS aggregate ends its initializer's
ordinary temporaries. Both omit static-initializer EH/count regions. Rust now
reproduces those outputs, including their frozen frame-storage behavior. The
TLS probes avoid dereferencing a frame temporary after the initializer returns.
These are course LowIR observations, not a new hosted ABI or runtime policy.

A separate declaration-time member probe found an obsolete trivial-storage
guard on `materialize_member_expression`. The existing constructor owner
already registers a completed nontrivial member's subobject cleanup. Removing
that guard lets the recorded materialization flow reach that owner, preserving
normal and exceptional recursive destruction.

### Frozen constructor-demand quirk

The added shutdown control passed its runtime assertions but initially failed
raw bytes: C++ emitted the referenced object's constructor before an unrelated
previously declared destructor. Read-only gdb breakpoints on the frozen
`ProgramLowering::demand_entry` and `emit_definition`, plus caller backtraces,
locate the demands in `global_lowering.cpp::define_global`.

The source explains this deterministic compatibility requirement:
`initializer_constructor_heads.find(nullptr)` returns the empty slot's zero
value once the frozen `PointerMap` has storage. The initializer chain at index
zero is the first recorded constructor. Therefore an absent initializer on a
static global can demand that constructor early. This is a reproducible frozen
baseline quirk, recorded here; no oracle, reference or acceptance policy was
changed. Raw gdb traces, `ordering-repro.cpp`, the source owners and independent
order-finding outputs are retained in the evidence root.

Rust represents the observation as a graph-owned **first `ConstructorId`**,
recorded with the initializer-constructor chains and consumed through a narrow
semantic accessor. It uses no null pointer keys, address-dependent ordering,
text lookup or fixture recognition. Instance member initializer patterns do not
publish this static-initializer fact; their constructors remain demanded by the
owning constructor body. Independent probes cover an unrelated early destructor,
a nested constructor argument and a default member initializer alone, preserving
their different emission orders and alias order.

The new executed PA11 control
`tests/controls/namespace-reference-temporary-finalization.t` checks construction,
reference/member/array bindings, conditional completion, default member
construction and final destruction **after main**. Its earliest global watcher
requires all objects destroyed in the expected order; an erroneous shutdown
writes to a null volatile pointer and fails the runtime control. The valid
program does not take that failure branch. Existing fixtures remain unchanged.

### Findings resolved in this audit increment

Stored objects with a `noexcept(false)` destructor previously returned status
**86** at `throwing storage destructor cleanup`, even if the destructor returned
normally. Initial protected probes record **10 failures of 14** in
`probes-entry.json[l]` and `probes-entry.log`. The failure covers ordinary locals,
arrays, globals and constructor/destructor subobjects, all within the handout's
nonvirtual declaration-time lifetime contract. Removing the rejection exposed
missing exceptional suffixes and terminate regions; this increment ports those
owners together.

- Normal lexical destruction protects remaining outer objects; exceptional
  destruction wraps a potentially throwing destructor in a terminate region.
  Shared return continuations retain their below-object call-unwind prefix.
  Lexical suffixes and call-unwind suffixes remain separate, with exact dispatch,
  handler selector, block placement and instruction ordering.
- Named arrays use the C++ guarded reverse progress loop for throwing elements.
  Default construction protects completed prefixes, including the exact small
  unroll/large loop boundary. Construction/deferred batches preserve the source's
  terminate-landing placement. Detached destructor bodies enter their subobject
  cleanup after popping the same records as C++.
- Automatic braced class arrays previously hit an additional trivial-storage
  guard. They now share typed aggregate initialization, counted prefixes and
  normal lexical destruction. Multidimensional lists retain nested row ownership
  and reusable row slots; helper initialization advances the same count. Counted
  unwind loops guard the entire reverse prefix and readdress named storage where
  the source does.
- Namespace/TLS array initialization accepts the same destructor contracts.
  Synthetic terminate landings construct typed pointer values without demanding
  a nonexistent source return type. The program-owned startup body preserves
  monotonically assigned selectors across successive initializers.

Required regressions are in PA11's executed controls lane:
`throwing-destructor-lexical-transfers.t`,
`throwing-destructor-subobjects.t` and
`throwing-destructor-static-arrays.t`. They exercise scope/return/loop/goto exits,
constructor/destructor bodies and bases, both array-size boundaries, aggregate
helpers, counted arrays, nested rows, global initialization and first-use TLS.
Existing compiler-input fixtures and frozen references are unchanged.

- `lower/object_lowering.cpp::subobject_bit_limit` and
  `lower/aggregate_lowering.cpp`: nonvirtual data limits and typed versus
  transfer views of bit-field reads/writes, including packed split accesses.
- `lower/function_lowering.cpp`, `lower/program_lowering.cpp` and
  `sema/semantics_dump.cpp`: synthesized body selection, emission demand/source
  order, parameter metadata and the hidden implicit-member presentation boundary.

The sole fixed failure diverged in semantic lookup: no implicit assignment
binding existed for the qualified base receiver. It now uses the same typed
public callable declarations and ordinary receiver/reference conversions as
explicit member and operator calls. The selected move entry consumes the
original value-initialized temporary; its emitted body copies the class's
storage and returns the original object pointer, byte for byte with C++.

`sema/assignment.rs` owns ordinary assignment classification, selection and
retained subobject/parameter-copy facts. It extends the existing explicit
`copy_properties.rs` deepest-first dependency walk: class completion classifies
written members and publishes written defaults, while ordinary implicit members
wait for a complete-class copy or assignment lookup demand. Pending/active/ready
states prevent repeated work and cycles. User-declared constructors, assignment
operators and destructors suppress implicit moves as in C++; deleted defaulted
moves are excluded before call ranking. Const/reference members, union variants,
access, mutable-source cv and selected subobjects determine the published facts.
Imported using functions retain their original owner/classification and do not
suppress a derived class's own implicit declarations.

`graph.rs` retains canonical function/type/binding identities, public access,
original versus implicit origin, parameter scopes and typed exception sources.
Synthetic assignment declarations have no fabricated syntax or semantic AST.
The original initialization/call expressions retain all selected receivers and
argument conversions. Ordinary/qualified/member/operator/using/address lookup
routes demand the members before reading their existing indexes. Reusable
analyzer-owned candidate scratch is restored after every query, including errors;
selection uses a winner pass and one verification pass over that name's set.
A unit regression checks unchanged syntax counts, untouched unused classes,
deepest-first selected child functions and empty retained query scratch.

`lower/assignment.rs` consumes each selected action without lookup or semantic
reanalyzing. It preserves whole/prefix transfer order, `nvsize` tail protection,
scalar source-before-destination access, base/member calls, empty-member demand,
scalar arrays, class-array unrolling through eight elements and indexed loops
above eight, trivial by-value parameter storage and the final `*this` return.
`bit_fields.rs` groups allocation units and shares the ordinary packed/split
access implementation. Transfer eligibility inspects adjacent overlapping
members and bases, respecting nonvirtual data limits, volatility and nontrivial
calls. The separate typed/transfer views preserve C++ signedness copies and
instruction order; no whole-class rescan occurs for every bit-field run.

`function.rs`/`lower/mod.rs` keep source, synthesized and external bodies
separate. Implicit entries use C++'s `other` parameter/slot and first-demand
order. Defaults written after the first declaration emit at their source
position and contribute an explicit layout boundary root even without a call
in that unit. Single-unit and multi-unit probes cover strong definitions,
inline coalescing, internal identities and duplicate-definition diagnostics.
Synthesized noexcept assignment bodies preserve C++ metadata without inventing
a function-level terminate region. Semantic dumps retain operator spelling and
omit hidden implicit assignment definitions, independently of LowIR demand.

The source map records the owning responsibilities and remaining boundaries.
There is no second syntax/semantic tree, text transport, fallback lookup,
whole-program polling, global invalidation, name-based semantic identity,
optimization, dependency, unsafe/FFI code, subprocess compiler implementation or
fixture recognition. Body locals, temporaries, array counters and LowIR belong
to the current function and are released by the existing streaming boundary.
Optimizer, register allocator and native code-generator sources are unchanged.

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen implementation
under `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp/dev/src` directly:

- `lower/tracked_lifetime_lowering.cpp`: guarded operand query, tracked segment,
  flag reset order, stable marker retargeting, completed cleanup-chain layout
  and lifetime extension at segment exit.
- `lower/cleanup_lowering.cpp`: full-expression and initializer boundaries,
  completion registration, split regions, conditional chain construction,
  region placement and destruction order.
- `lower/condition_lowering.cpp`: conditional glvalue operands, root versus
  nested scalar/void/logical branches and branch-local destruction.
- `lower/object_lowering.cpp`: temporary materialization, tracked storage,
  scoped extension, flag tests/clears and constructor argument staging.
- `lower/function_lowering.h` and `lowir/builder.cpp`: function-local transient
  records, captured instruction positions and separate block layout.
- `sema/semantics_dump.cpp::constructor_action`: source argument subtree order.

Both initial failures were clustered at their earliest divergent owners.
The conditional reached the Rust lowering's explicit guarded-temporary stub,
after semantic copy properties had succeeded; the assignment case still stops
at implicit member lookup. The implemented group follows the former boundary
through completion, ordinary cleanup and scoped extension, without changing
semantic constructor selection or the latter lookup boundary.

`lower/tracked_lifetimes.rs` owns one current full-expression's tracking state
inside the current function. Its bounded query reads original ExprIds and
retained value categories; a reusable iterative worklist has no allocation per
visited node. When any temporary must outlive its branch, every temporary in
that expression receives a flag, set after construction and cleared before its
cleanup call. Captured storage Values preserve the original slot identity so
cleanup blocks retake addresses without reading a temporary defined only in
another branch. Ordinary references extend the selected cleanup and guard to
their own scope. Unbranched extensions recover their original address when
tracking ends; retained extensions invalidate only their own chain memo fields.

`lower/lifetime.rs` owns the completed cleanup records and suffix chains.
A temporary landing is a placeholder while operands are emitted. Once the
expression finishes, stable region positions are retargeted to the complete
conditional chain and its blocks are placed immediately after the expression's
first block, restoring the original insertion point and pending cleanup state.
Tests, destructors and reverse destruction retain exact order. Tracked regions
remain open across the guarded destruction dispatch; nonthrowing calls still
receive the C++ segment markers and scalar spills. Multiple expressions and
separate emitted functions do not retain tracking contents.

`lowir/builder.rs` exposes narrow typed insertion, region retargeting, addressed
storage and layout-restoration operations over private stores. Flag resets are
collected and inserted as one reversed group, rotating the instruction sequence
once rather than shifting it once per temporary. Stable instruction and value
definition IDs and use counts survive insertion. A meaningful builder regression
validates those properties, the completed region target, cleanup layout and
restored insertion with the ordinary LowIR validator.

`conditions.rs`/`expressions.rs` distinguish root branch-local scalar/void/logical
temporaries from guarded nested operands. Conditions avoid empty cleanup blocks
when their root branches already destroyed every temporary. `construction.rs`
and `function.rs` retain constructor initializer staging: a single temporary-
making expression resets flags before the object's address, while a multiple-
argument constructor resets them after that address. `materialization.rs`
consumes the existing selected constructor and copy facts without new analysis.
`semantics_dump.rs` schedules constructor arguments in reverse source order on
its existing worklist, so one argument's subtree completes before the next
argument is rendered; probes exposed the former interleaving with conditionals.

The source map records all owning responsibilities and remaining boundaries.
There is no new syntax/semantic tree, semantic lookup or reanalysis in lowering,
text transport, name-based identity, whole-program query, global invalidation,
backend lifetime change, optimization, dependency, lint suppression, unsafe/FFI
implementation, subprocess compiler fallback or source/test recognition.

### Typed data flow, responsibilities and architecture review

`sema/copy_properties.rs` owns unit-local `CopyPropertiesId` records and a
private store. Explicit dependency frames visit only a demanded class's base
and non-reference member classes, including array elements, deepest first.
Class preparation has distinct Pending/Active/Ready states; successful facts
are reused. A 512-class ownership regression proves one successful copy/move
property pair per class, stable storage across repeat demand, unchanged parsed
expression cardinality, no constructor/layout demand and isolation of an
unrelated class. Completed class structure is immutable; no global generation,
retry polling, name scan over unrelated declarations or cache invalidation is
introduced. Frames die when the demand finishes; canonical properties die with
Graph.

The records retain selected constructor BindingIds or implicit class/kind
identities, their canonical reference conversions and original base/member
EntityIds. Transient typed source facts supply cv and lvalue/xvalue category to
the shared conversion/ranking service; no expression or syntax node is created.
The constructor-property group includes:

- implicit copy const-source parameter choice, copy/move declaration suppression,
  deleted defaulted move omission, no subobject selection for an implicit copy
  already deleted by a written move, and correct distinction between a written
  destructor and an implicitly declared nontrivial destructor;
- array/object cv, mutable source qualification, rvalue reference member copy
  deletion, union nontrivial variant deletion and subobject destructor usability;
- selected constructor deleted/access checks, retaining the selected identity
  even when access deletes the enclosing member, protected base versus member
  object restrictions, friend access, explicit copy constructors and later
  default arguments;
- triviality and nonthrowing facts, including the frozen assumption that later
  default arguments can throw, explicit exception specification precedence and
  after-first defaulted definition diagnostics;
- defaulted written constructor facts at class completion while that class's
  implicit constructor candidates still wait for a use; inherited constructors
  are excluded from copy-member classification.

`copy_initialization.rs` consumes these facts for selected-use deletion, elision,
trivial storage transfers and class parameter ABI. Nontrivial implicit/defaulted
copies reach the explicit missing-body boundary instead of becoming byte copies.
`construction.rs` consumes the retained properties for after-first defaulted
copy definitions. Written defaulted trivial-copy selections retain their odr-use
edge separately from actual LowIR calls; elided selections do not add it.
`semantics_dump.rs` renders recorded nonelided constructor actions and their
original operands through its iterative worklist, with the required separate
trailing declaration. Five adjacent dump mismatches found during probes were
resolved in this group.

The additional property regression checks selected subobject identity, recursive
const/deleted/trivial/throw facts, protected access, unions, references, mutable
nonconst-overload preference, explicit `noexcept(false)` precedence, selected
identity retention after inaccessible subobject selection and suppressed-copy
selection isolation.
`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the translated owners. The original AST,
canonical TypeIds, use conversions and demand ownership remain shared; there is
no second semantic tree, textual transport, semantic reanalysis in lowering,
implicit callable body fabrication, speculative whole-program work, new
allocation on repeated completed-fact lookup or lifetime change in backend
scratch. No optimizer, register allocator, native backend, dependency, lint
waiver, unsafe/FFI code, compiler subprocess, environment read, fixture/checker/
oracle policy or Ralph state was changed. The file audit caught an overlong new
unit-input literal; its Rust line-continuation representation now preserves
exactly the same source bytes, with no audit exception.

### Frozen owners, typed flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen C++ sources under
`/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp/dev/src` directly:
`sema/analyzer_calls.cpp::arguments`, `sema/analyzer_copy.cpp`,
`lower/expression_lowering.cpp::push_argument/push_converted`,
`lower/object_lowering.cpp::initialize_from/call_special/call_constructor`,
`lower/aggregate_lowering.cpp::copy_object`,
`lower/allocation_lowering.cpp::allocation`,
`lower/cleanup_lowering.cpp::begin_full_expression/note_call_arguments/
begin_call/emit_call/creates_temporaries`,
`lower/tracked_lifetime_lowering.cpp::branch_temporaries`,
`lower/statement_lowering.cpp`'s condition/return contexts and
`lower/symbol_lowering.cpp::passed_directly/copied_trivially`.

The gained case's first divergence was argument storage, not copy-constructor
selection: Rust passed the original class expression as an ordinary operand;
the frozen course path allocates an `arg` object, computes its address,
initializes it and passes that address. The oracle does not select a copy
constructor or register destruction of this ellipsis copy. This includes
lvalues of classes with user-provided or deleted copy constructors; substituting
the declared class-parameter path would change observable output.

- `sema/procedural_calls.rs` retains the original qualified class target and
  existing scalar default promotions, recording the ellipsis rank once on the
  original operand. As in C++, a missing ordinary implicit sequence retains a
  typed target/result-only conversion. Complete class argument storage enters
  the existing body-owned layout request queue. Unevaluated calls make no
  request; unused inline bodies do not force layout. An incomplete class stays
  at the ordinary lowering storage diagnostic boundary. No copy member is
  fabricated or demanded for the ellipsis operand.
- `lower/materialization.rs::ellipsis_argument` owns caller argument storage:
  a generated `arg` slot and address precede source evaluation. A glvalue's
  address is evaluated once, including effects for an empty source; a nonempty
  source copies its owned nonvirtual bytes with exact alignment. A prvalue
  initializes that storage through the existing selected constructor,
  aggregate or result materialization services. The copied argument itself
  adds no destructor action. Ordinary nested expression temporaries keep
  their existing cleanup ownership.
- Ordinary direct, indirect, member and functor calls in `lower/expressions.rs`,
  selected constructor surplus arguments in `lower/construction.rs`, and
  variadic placement operands in `lower/allocation.rs` share that helper.
  Fixed parameter/default/reference argument behavior remains separately typed.
  This completes the course argument-transfer path for the ordinary storage
  forms exercised below, rather than recognizing the gained empty-class test.
- `lower/temporary_scan.rs` distinguishes `Ordinary`, `Value` and `ClassResult`
  full-expression uses. Caller-owned result roots are excluded from temporary
  counting; their operands are still visited. Allocation placement and
  initializer operands participate in the existing iterative scan.
  Function-owned `TemporarySegment::{Empty,Delayed,Hidden,Eager}` in
  `lower/lifetime.rs` separates scanned temporaries from temporary reference-
  binding scratch and records whether an ordinary call's operands permit eager
  hidden regions. It resets at the owning full-expression boundary.
- `lower/lifetime_regions.rs` consumes cached operand summaries and call marks
  to open the hidden region before a throwing call's arguments, including an
  empty cleanup chain. A nonthrowing outer call can leave its throwing argument
  constructor to open the region. Selected constructors retain balanced call
  marks. Scalar results spill/read back across genuine temporary segments even
  before any object has become alive; reference-binding scratch alone does not
  acquire those spills. Delayed segments retain their distinct region behavior.
  `statements.rs`, `conditions.rs`, `class_results.rs` and `function.rs` pass
  these typed contexts; they perform no semantic reanalysis.

Two unit regressions establish unchanged parsed expression cardinality,
canonical qualified operand/target identity through parentheses, no ellipsis
copy action/member demand, and layout isolation for emitted, unused-inline and
unevaluated uses. No new owning syntax/semantic tree or graph identity exists.
The layout requests die with Analyzer; cached scans, arguments, temporary
segments, call marks, cleanup chains and backend scratch belong to one function.
Queries visit required operands and use the existing immutable-fact cache,
without unrelated declaration scans, global invalidation or speculative work.
Only typed LowIR crosses frontend release. `docs/port/source-map.tsv` records
all translated responsibilities. No dependency, lint waiver, unsafe/FFI code,
subprocess implementation, environment read, fixture recognition, protected
input/checker change, optimization/register allocator/backend change or Ralph
state edit was introduced. Development preservation checks exposed reference
scratch spills and caller-result root counting; the final contexts fix those
regressions, with zero lost fixed identities in the final result.

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and the current stage ledger. Consulted frozen
`/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp/dev/src` owners directly:
`lower/local_static_lowering.cpp::local_static/local_static_name`,
`symbol_lowering.cpp::abi_scope`,
`global_lowering.cpp::static_global/readonly_object/changeable_class/
static_element/missing_element/zero_value_initialized`,
`constant_lowering.cpp::initialization_value/constant_placements/constant_data`
and `sema/constant_construction.cpp::construct/value_initialize`.

Raw protected diffs established two static-storage responsibilities:
constructor entries duplicated one source static declaration, and an empty
initialized derived class incorrectly acquired a dynamic startup initializer.
The readonly mutable/volatile/destructor eligibility was already correct.

- `lower/symbols.rs` now caches `EntityId -> StorageSymbols` per semantic unit.
  Every ABI entry executing the original constructor/destructor body reuses
  the source variable's object, guard, binding, spelling and discriminator.
  `lower/local_storage.rs` publishes each static global, initializer image and
  guard only once, while preserving the first-entry guard instructions in
  each emitted function body. Constant/dynamic scalars, arrays, string-backed
  images and TLS share this path. Distinct source declarations, nested same-name
  declarations and overloads keep distinct identities. The frozen tool emits
  `C1` in the observed local object name; the fixture's explanatory `C4`
  comment does not replace that observable oracle spelling.
- `sema/static_arrays.rs` uses the declaration's original empty braced
  initializer and selected trivial, non-user-provided constructor facts to
  request a typed evaluated zero image. An omitted nonaggregate subobject
  with a ready trivial default constructor instead contributes zero bytes,
  as the frozen `missing_element` path does. Nontrivial construction stays
  dynamic; TLS preserves the frozen exclusion of evaluated class initialization.
- `sema/static_objects.rs` completes the requested layout, then traverses
  canonical base/member offsets into query-owned typed placements. Stable
  offset ordering, supported bit-unit deduplication and merged zero gaps
  preserve the C++ constant-data convention. Empty bases add no occupied data;
  scalar arithmetic zeros remain scalar items, pointers and padding remain
  byte zeros, arrays repeat their element offsets and a union uses its first
  active member. Completing layout before traversal also handles private,
  defaulted nonaggregate classes whose member offsets were not yet demanded.
- `lower/globals.rs` consumes successful constant images even when a declaration
  selected a trivial constructor. The object publishes its constant image and
  does not acquire a synthetic startup action. Existing readonly qualification
  still checks mutable/volatile members and nontrivial destruction recursively.

The local-static cache has one immutable semantic-unit key space and dies with
`Symbols`; the cross-unit ABI symbol table retains its existing lifetime.
Constant placement vectors die with their initialization query; work visits
only the requested type's subobjects, with O(n log n) placement ordering and
O(n) scratch, independent of unrelated declarations. All original expressions
and constructor plans remain shared by compact IDs. Lowering does not rerun
lookup, select constructors, synthesize syntax or parse text. Only typed LowIR
crosses frontend release, and function/backend scratch retains its original
lifetime. No dependency, lint waiver, optimizer/register allocator/backend,
protected fixture/reference/checker or Ralph state changed.
`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records each translated responsibility.

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this stage ledger. Consulted the frozen
`/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp/dev/src` owners directly:
`lower/cleanup_lowering.cpp::begin_full_expression/begin_initializer/
caller_destroyed/creates_temporaries/temporary_region/temporary_alive/
destroy_temporaries/emit_call`,
`object_lowering.cpp::local_object/materialize/materialize_prvalue/temporary_object`,
`expression_lowering.cpp::push_converted/bind_converted/bind_extended/
extends_temporary/extend_temporary`, `function_lowering.cpp::lower/
lower_dynamic_initializer`, `statement_lowering.cpp::local_initializer/
condition_branch`, `discard_lowering.cpp::discard`,
`condition_lowering.cpp::choice_operand/logical_step` and
`tracked_lifetime_lowering.cpp::branch_temporaries/guarded_temporaries`.
The latter owners define the next boundary, not a completed flag-tracking port.

The two gained cases first diverged at the absent temporary cleanup path.
The complete ordinary, non-branch-created ownership group now includes:

- `lower/materialization.rs` distinguishes argument, reference, designator and
  discarded uses with `TemporaryUse`. A successfully constructed temporary
  retains its address in a `Temporary(Value)` cleanup. Even an inert nontrivial
  destructor runs for a temporary. Completion splits the current unwind region
  and extends its chain in construction order. Failed construction never adds
  a completed object. Discarded operator/literal class results enter this path;
  ordinary scalar and reference operator results retain their existing calls.
- `lower/temporary_scan.rs` walks original typed operands and selected defaults,
  excluding unevaluated operands and synthetic intrinsic callees. Its sparse
  ExprId summaries and reusable work vector belong to one Function, against
  immutable unit facts; no name/type rendering, lookup, semantic reselection or
  syntax reconstruction occurs. Constructor staging preserves the C++ precedence
  between a single temporary-making expression and caller-owned parameter forcing.
  Noexcept calls, literal/operator results and parameter destruction retain the
  frozen segment/spill rules; no new optimization is introduced.
- `lower/lifetime.rs` destroys transient objects in reverse completion order at
  their full-expression boundary. Removed entries cannot remain in a retained
  scoped owner's unwind/return cache; compacted cleanup depths are repaired.
  Scalar/object declarations, discarded expressions, returns and condition values
  drain their temporaries. A branching condition uses distinct true/false cleanup
  blocks before reaching the selected statement. Return values are already computed
  before temporary destruction and ordinary lexical cleanup.
- Selected converting-reference temporaries join the same list, then transfer to
  the original declaring owner when a local reference or aggregate reference member
  extends them. `sema/lowering.rs::lower_reference_binding` reads the retained
  outer user-conversion target before the constructor argument's first conversion,
  so liveness/shared-return scans retain the actual reference owner. Class-parameter
  aggregate reference members clear the surrounding named/static extension context:
  their temporaries belong to the call's full-expression.
- Class parameters consume completed canonical passing facts. Direct parameters
  use their object bytes; indirect parameters use a caller-created address and
  state `ptr`, `pass=by_address` and `object_bytes` on definitions, declarations
  and function-pointer signatures. The callee retains the course's unused object
  frame slot while designating the incoming address, and never destroys the
  caller's parameter. Supplied/default, direct/indirect, member, operator and
  ordinary constructor calls share the initialization path.
- Namespace and TLS initializer contexts destroy transient temporaries before
  advancing to the next declaration or completing the TLS guard. They retain the
  frozen absence of local full-expression exception regions. Persistent destructible
  temporaries still return status 86 at their separate finalization boundary.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the translated responsibilities. The existing
ownership invariant test now also mixes scoped and transient temporaries in
class-argument and aggregate initialization, retaining distinct declaring owners,
empty temporary-extension scratch and unchanged original syntax cardinality.
All cleanup records, block/position IDs, result spill state and temporary-query
scratch end with one Function. Unit-owned class/ABI memos retain their existing
lifetimes; only typed LowIR crosses frontend release. There is no new dependency,
unsafe/FFI code, compiler fallback, fixture recognition, environment read, lint
waiver, optimizer/register-allocator/backend change or protected input/checker
or Ralph-state edit.

`cleanup_lowering.cpp::emit_call/zero_return/cleanup_chain`, and
`syntax/ast_statements.h::LiveThen/LiveElse`.

The four gained cases diverged first at missing class-call materialization,
class-return storage, or eager storage-type demand for an incomplete class
return declaration. The port follows those shared result owners:

- `sema/layout.rs` extends the existing emitted-signature demand worklist to
  prepare complete class return layout and canonical copy-call properties.
  It follows existing function/constructor demand edges, leaving incomplete
  named return classes and unrelated classes untouched. The new unit invariant
  checks unchanged syntax cardinality, demanded alias/canonical result facts,
  and absent layout/copy facts for unused and incomplete classes.
- `lower/class_results.rs` owns direct result copies, function-owned `retobj`
  storage, caller-provided indirect storage, named return elision, and typed
  zero-result continuations. `symbols.rs` consumes existing canonical class
  copy facts and the memoized ABI quarter classification in `class_parameters.rs`.
  Nontrivial, large or MEMORY class results use a hidden destination. Direct
  SSE/mixed results retain exact serialized result classes; an unmodeled direct
  result states none. Incomplete return declarations state `void` without layout
  or destructor-property demand. An actual incomplete call retains the frozen
  structured lowering diagnostic, rather than an internal invariant failure.
- Definitions, declarations and function-pointer call signatures put an indirect
  result first, followed by `this` and written parameters. Source and unnamed
  positional parameters retain their proper shifted indices and boundary facts.
  Ordinary, operator and text/character literal calls all pass a destination or
  copy a direct result into it. Class results never receive scalar call spills.
  Existing argument conversions and selected-call identities remain authoritative.
- Named result elision scans only an indirect-result function's original body,
  once. The resolved Id expressions, canonical class IDs, automatic-storage
  properties and lexical ScopeIds determine whether every return names one
  eligible object. Its initialization uses the incoming address directly, with
  no invented local slot or `addr` instruction. Successful returns transfer that
  object's ownership; unwind and other scope exits keep its existing cleanup.
  Distinct locals and by-value parameters preserve ordinary storage-copy paths.
- Direct returns share one `retobj` across all return sites and shared lexical
  cleanup continuations. Nonthrowing-function termination continuations zero a
  fresh object slot for a direct result, and return `void` for an indirect one.
  Empty functional/braced result actions, comma effects, and computed-address
  aggregate array element conversions preserve the frozen LowIR detail.
  Named aggregate roots retain their separate initialization conversion rules.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records each translated responsibility. All storage,
return-scan scratch, result IDs and cleanup state are function-owned; ABI class
memos remain unit-owned. Typed LowIR alone crosses frontend release. There is no
new lookup, semantic selection, syntax cloning, reparsing, whole-program hot-path
scan, dependency, unsafe/FFI code, compiler fallback, fixture recognition,
environment read, lint waiver, optimizer/register-allocator/backend change,
protected input/checker change or Ralph state change.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,

global_allocation_functions/usual_deallocation`,

Both gained fixed cases diverged first at missing allocation semantics.

- `sema/allocation.rs` uses indexed class/global special-name lookup, ordinary
  overload ranking with a typed `size_t` prvalue prefix, and original placement
  ExprIds. Selected allocation/deallocation BindingIds, target TypeId,
  ConstructorId/argument shape and reserved-placement identity live in the
  sparse original-expression fact store. No fabricated size-expression syntax,
  cloned AST, or parallel semantic tree is introduced. The unit regression
  checks unchanged syntax cardinality, source-versus-converted argument types,
  selected signature, separate constructor actions and allocation-only layout.
- `procedural_calls.rs` shares ordinary candidate comparison, argument
  application, class-copy/destruction checking and base-path recording.
  Candidate scratch remains Analyzer-owned, restores empty contents on every
  Result path and retains capacity. Deleted/access checks precede selected
  application; losing candidates publish no construction use. Allocation
  function templates retain an explicit incomplete deduction boundary.
- Allocation checks preserve object/incomplete/const diagnostics and matching
  deallocation access. `layout.rs` queues the allocated target's storage through
  the existing owner/demand worklist, including when no later member access
  requests that layout. Unevaluated allocations retain semantic facts without
  creating body demand or a storage request. No unrelated layout/body scan.
- `lower/allocation.rs` consumes `AllocationView` directly, emits the size's
  exact i32-to-i64 extension (or large i64 constant), evaluates written placement
  operands before initialization, and uses the selected allocation result as
  the object address. Reserved global placement forms skip the null branch;
  nonthrowing class/custom placement forms test null before initialization.
  Scalar, aggregate helper/memberwise, default/value/selected constructor paths
  reuse their owning routines. Allocated objects gain no automatic destructor.
  Reference-member bindings use full-expression ownership, not an enclosing
  automatic aggregate's extension owner.
- Two deterministic frozen details are preserved: scalar allocation initializes
  through an ordinary typed Place even for a volatile target, and the allocation
  call loop emits only written placement operands, even when overload viability
  accepted an unwritten default. `volatile-scalar.cpp` and
  `placement-default.cpp` retain exact raw receipts. This is not permission to
  repair the oracle's behavior. The latter is a raw-only probe because the
  frozen emitted call omits a parameter. `ordinary-null.cpp` is also raw-only:
  the frozen globally predeclared allocation path emits an unconditional store;
  its initial native observation signaled. `big-size.cpp` checks large size
  constants without attempting to run against undersized storage.
- Semantic presentation preserves the frozen generic `expression` header and
  allocated pointer type. Semantic throw queries consume selected calls and
  original operands; lowering separately accounts for unwritten constructor
  defaults when deciding whether initialization needs deallocation cleanup.
  Required failure cleanup remains explicit status 86, never silently omitted.

frontend release. No dependency, unsafe/FFI code, compiler fallback, fixture

(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged.
This is a coherent partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**,

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and the current ledger. Consulted the frozen sources
under `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp/dev/src` directly:
`sema/analyzer_initialization.cpp::braced_argument/list_sequence/
aggregate_accepts/members_accept/elements_accept/element_accepts/
list_constructor/list_argument/implicit_constructor`, `sema/aggregates.h`,
`analyzer_calls.cpp::implicit_sequence/convert_arguments`,
`analyzer_construction.cpp::initialize_class/select_constructor`,
`conversions.cpp::compare`, `lower/object_lowering.cpp::array_temporary`,
`aggregate_lowering.cpp::aggregate_array_object/helper_eligible/
structured_item/structured_array`, and `expression_lowering.cpp::bind_converted`.

All three entry failures diverged during braced argument ranking. Aggregate
class arguments stopped at missing implementation; class-array arguments used
standard-only scalar sequences, rejecting viable converting constructors.
The existing array cv helper already followed element qualifiers and is unchanged.

- `list_arguments.rs` walks the original ExprId child slices with a cursor.
  Aggregate viability follows member declaration order, unions' first named
  members, unnamed-bit-field skipping, nested arrays and class brace elision.
  A same-class expression or string array initializes a whole subaggregate;
  other expressions enter its children. No narrowing, omitted-member diagnostic,
  access/deletion check, object fact or constructor demand is published during
  candidate ranking. Empty subaggregate array siblings cannot consume another
  initializer; their repeated zero-consumption query is bounded without
  expanding the target array or changing initialization/emission.
- Array lists retain their worst ordinary implicit argument sequence, including
  converting constructors and nested class lists, then restore the outer array
  result/reference target. Aggregate class lists have user-defined rank.
  Ordinary nonaggregate lists query their owning constructor index, with the
  empty-list default path separate. `ConversionConstructor` distinguishes
  declared BindingId and implicit class EntityId identities without creating
  ConstructorId plans. Different identities tie; one identity preserves the
  existing cv/ref preferences. Ranking scratch is Analyzer-owned and cleared
  on every Result return, retaining capacity for subsequent queries.
- `procedural_calls.rs` separates candidate ranking from the final diagnostic.
  `ArgumentSequence` carries the list constructor identity through enclosing
  call/operator selection. `initialize_ranked_list_argument` passes the selected
  declaration into `ClassInput`; constructor application prepares that callable's
  argument conversions without repeating constructor overload selection. Only
  the chosen use records conversions, constructor plans and demand. Ordinary
  value-list calls check parameter destruction at the frozen preconstruction
  point. `initialization.rs` retains reference-bound class cv on the original
  list object, preserving semantic dumps as well as LowIR bytes.
- `arrays.rs` gives temporary aggregate class arrays the existing automatic
  array helper-eligibility path with an absent named owner. The predicate rejects
  nonaggregates and whole-class source expressions before helper formation.
  Both helper and memberwise paths retain the common scoped temporary cleanup
  registration. `aggregates.rs` consumes a string inside a braced character
  array as literal code units, preserving field/element projection and volatile
  stores. This avoids an invalid string-address store and unwanted literal
  global; no optimizer transformation was added.
- A Rust unit regression exercises aggregate, declared-constructor, implicit
  default and converting class-array queries; verifies stable expression count,
  original source/list facts, separate conversions, absent losing constructor
  plans/body demand; and checks selected constructor identity and target cv
  after application. The original syntax is never cloned, reparsed or rewritten.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records these owners and responsibilities. Only typed
semantic IDs and facts reach lowering; function slots, paths, helper arguments
and cleanup state retain their existing per-function lifetimes. Only typed LowIR
crosses frontend release. No dependency, unsafe code, FFI, compiler fallback,
fixture recognition, text transport, environment read, blanket lint waiver,
optimizer/register-allocator/backend change, protected file or Ralph state edit.

`measure.py`, `resources.log`, `resources.json`, `scaling.jsonl` and `scaling/`
retain generated supported inputs and every sample. Each shape calls one shared
function from 100/400/1,600 functions: a brace-elided aggregate reference or a
reference to two converting class-array elements. Each runs Rust/Rust and
C++/C++ calibration followed by two alternating ABBA blocks. All six protected
receipts and measured LowIR hashes match. Binaries are frozen by
`binary-hashes.json`; the Rust hash matches `final.json`. Samples include

**16 MiB**. Raw initial/qualified attempts remain under `stack-qualifications/`.

or diagnostic position. The genuine byte-budget guard is unchanged.

reference/return controls stop at class call materialization; cast/global
controls stop at full-expression cleanup. Class-subobject copy/move plans are a

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen implementation
under `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp/dev/src` directly:
`sema/analyzer_conversions.cpp::user_conversion/user_candidate_order/
apply_user_conversion`, `analyzer_calls.cpp::implicit_sequence/convert_arguments`,
`analyzer_operators.cpp::operator_conversions`,
`analyzer_construction.cpp::check_binding_temporary`,
`conversions.cpp::compare`, `analyzer_exceptions.cpp::conversion_may_throw`,
`analyzer_member_pointers.cpp::check_conversion_access`,
`semantics_dump.cpp::conversion_node`,
`lower/object_lowering.cpp::call_constructor/initialize_from`,
`expression_lowering.cpp::push_converted/bind_converted`,
`conversion_lowering.cpp`, and `cleanup_lowering.cpp::may_throw`.

The three entry failures diverged during implicit argument conversion, after
ordinary/associated lookup. Their shared missing owner was the constructor
branch of `user_conversion`; no name lookup, ADL or string-pointer rule changed.

- `user_conversions.rs` owns ordinary non-template constructor candidate queries.
  It visits only the target class's declared constructor index, filters explicit
  constructors and arity/default availability, ranks the first **standard-only**
  conversion, and requires a unique best candidate. Deleted/inaccessible
  candidates participate. Query scratch belongs to the Analyzer; every return
  clears its contents and retains capacity. Ranking creates no callable plan,
  body demand or target layout demand and never recursively chains constructors.
- `ArgumentSequence` retains the selected BindingId and first standard step
  separately from the outer user-defined rank/result/reference sequence.
  Standard sequences beat user-defined ones; user-defined beats ellipsis.
  Different constructor identities tie, while one constructor's reference
  sequences retain cv/ref preference. Ordinary calls, constructor calls and
  overloaded operators share this ranking. Indirect calls, default arguments
  and reference initialization share selected-use application through the
  existing scalar conversion entry.
- Application checks deletion, access, destruction and final-copy viability at
  the frozen owner's point, then records constructor demand. Typed
  `ConversionSite` preserves the observable precedence difference: ordinary
  calls check parameter/binding destruction before applying the conversion;
  operators check the selected callable first. Discarded candidates are never
  applied or demanded. The two conflicting-deletion/access probes originally
  exposed this distinction and now match exactly.
- `expression_facts.rs` extends the existing private sparse ExprId-root
  `ClassConversion` store with the outer Conversion. It retains the selected
  ConstructorId and original argument ExprId; original source TypeId/category
  remain intact and its first standard conversion stays on its own facts.
  Parentheses alias one action; dense ExprFacts does not grow. The semantic
  printer reads the recorded outer conversion and does not expose the first
  step as an outer cast. A unit regression checks parse-once expression count,
  source identity/category/type, alias sharing, both conversion steps, selected
  constructor identity and absence of a losing constructor's plan/demand.
- `materialization.rs` consumes that action for caller-owned direct class
  arguments. Reference binding creates the frozen `arg`/`tmpobj` or persistent
  storage and calls the selected complete constructor once. One Function-local
  ExprId scopes consumption of the source's first conversion, restoring it on
  every Result return so nested defaults can use their own actions. There is
  no semantic graph mutation, synthetic syntax or repeated overload resolution.
  Existing constructor/default evaluation order, parameter bytes, ABI entry
  identity and definition demand remain in their owning paths.

Two nonstandard details follow the readable oracle exactly. Its converting
constructor's first standard base route is retained without the access check
used on an outer base conversion (`private-base-source.cpp`); the ordinary call
completion therefore does not recheck it. Its semantic and lowering throw
queries inspect the conversion's selected callable but do not add trailing
constructor default arguments (`noexcept-default.cpp`). Both raw reproducer
receipts and the corresponding C++ owners are retained; neither behavior is
silently corrected. No exhaustion depth or diagnostic position is calibrated.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the responsibilities and Rust owners. Sparse
use records die with their semantic unit; candidate scratch dies with Analyzer;
source-conversion selection, slots and call scratch die with one Function.
Only typed LowIR crosses frontend release. No new dependency, unsafe code,
FFI, compiler subprocess implementation, fixture recognition, text transport,
reparse, blanket lint waiver, environment read, optimizer, register allocator,
backend, fixture, oracle, protected policy or Ralph state was changed.

Implementation base HEAD: `d1d891c2c7103545649f998e038b06a1e3e03a49`
(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged.
This is a coherent partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**, phase
**implement**. **PA11 remains incomplete**; Ralph owns acceptance, final audits
and advancement. The preceding goal turn was **progress**, established by its
committed conditional/designator implementation and two fixed-manifest gains.
This turn is **progress**: **383 to 388 passing**, **26 to 21 failing**, on the
same **409 fixed identities**, with **zero lost passing identities**. The
protected 409/411 reporting defect remains independently reproducible.

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen implementation
under `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp` directly:
`sema/analyzer_copy.cpp`, `analyzer_construction.cpp::copy_initialization/
record_copy/initialize_class`, `analyzer_calls.cpp::convert_arguments/
argument_dependent`, `sema/aggregates.h::InitializesSubaggregate`,
`lower/object_lowering.cpp::call_special/call_constructor/initialize_from/
materialize_prvalue/local_object`, `aggregate_lowering.cpp::copy_object/
aggregate_object/aggregate_stores/materialize_member`,
`expression_lowering.cpp::side_effect_free`, and
`symbol_lowering.cpp`'s copy/move parameter boundary facts.

Raw entry traces show that the ADL-suppression, associated-namespace,
using-source-point and nested enclosing-type cases stop at nonelided class copy
construction, after lookup. Their shared leaf-class construction owner is ported
here; no ADL rule was changed to recognize these cases.

- `copy_initialization.rs` records `CopyUse::Object` versus `Value`, retaining
  whether explicit constructors participate and whether prvalues initialize in
  place. Original source types, categories and operand conversions remain intact.
  `class_copy` retains selected implicit/declared origin and reference sequence.
  Nonelided actions independently name a reference TypeId for storage transfer or
  a checked ConstructorId for a call. Selection/deletion/access checks precede
  action/body demand. Elided selections create no body demand.
- `expression_facts.rs` owns nonelided actions in a private sparse map keyed by
  the original ExprId root. Parentheses share one record. Dense ExprFacts size
  does not grow. `ClassInitialization::FromExpression` distinguishes the
  original functional/explicit cast operand from the copy action on that operand;
  forwarding an expression no longer implies that every copy was elided.
- The completed storage group covers ordinary leaf classes with scalar,
  pointer, reference, scalar-array and bit-field members, implicit copy/move and
  first-declaration-defaulted copy selection. Existing constructor entries also
  handle selected user-provided copy/move bodies and their default arguments.
  Direct class parameter boundaries remain governed by the existing canonical
  class property; nontrivial caller-owned parameter lifetime remains incomplete.
- `materialization.rs` consumes the recorded reference binding and emits
  `copyobj` for the smaller of object size and nonvirtual data size, with the
  original alignment. Empty copies write nothing. Object-construction uses may
  skip an effect-free empty source; a class argument still computes its source
  address, and calls with effects are evaluated once. No implicit callable body
  is demanded for an ordinary storage copy.
- `aggregates.rs` preserves named automatic reference-cast initialization as an
  untyped object load/store before taking the destination address, including
  volatile destination writes. It also applies the frozen `aggregate_stores`
  early return for named aggregates with no stored fields: the inherited rule
  can omit empty member initializer effects. Nonaggregate constructor members
  still take their ordinary calls. No new optimization was introduced.
- `function.rs` consumes the recorded semantic copy kind to mark a copy/move
  constructor's destination and first explicit source parameter `noalias`, for
  declarations and definitions. Further default parameters and assignment
  operators do not gain that fact.
- A semantic ownership regression checks parse-once expression count, shared
  parenthesis root, canonical source type/category, unchanged operand conversion,
  selected reference target and sparse action identity. Differential probes
  exercise the observable lowering; the unit test checks a phase invariant.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the C++ responsibilities and Rust owners.
Copy property memoization stays class-owned; copy/call scratch belongs to one
Analyzer and is reset after each query; sparse source facts die with the semantic
unit. Slots, expressions and call scratch stay with one Function, and only typed
LowIR crosses frontend release. New work visits only the source/target class and
selected constructor, never unrelated declarations. No dependency, unsafe code,
FFI, subprocess compiler implementation, fixture recognition, text transport,
syntax synthesis/reparse, environment read, blanket lint waiver, optimizer,
register allocator, backend, fixture, oracle, protected checker or Ralph state
was changed.

`scaling/` retain the exact inputs, output hashes and every timing/RSS sample.
Storage-copy inputs each declare a local leaf copy from a reference parameter;
class-argument inputs each pass a leaf glvalue to a shared value-parameter body.
Both use one canonical class. Each shape at 100/400/1,600 functions runs
Rust/Rust and C++/C++ calibration followed by two alternating ABBA blocks.
All six protected receipts and all measured output hashes match.
Times include process startup and concurrent gate work. These are observations,
not PA35/generated-code performance requirements.

| Shape / functions | Source bytes | Rust mean ms / peak RSS KiB | C++ mean ms / peak RSS KiB |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| storage-copies, 100 | 3341 | 20.45 / 8000 | 6.33 / 8096 |
| storage-copies, 400 | 13541 | 31.24 / 8744 | 11.17 / 9552 |
| storage-copies, 1600 | 54941 | 84.50 / 17032 | 30.84 / 14452 |
| class-arguments, 100 | 3141 | 19.04 / 8752 | 6.63 / 8080 |
| class-arguments, 400 | 12741 | 28.95 / 8756 | 10.70 / 9516 |
| class-arguments, 1600 | 51741 | 81.83 / 14316 | 25.69 / 13004 |

For 16x functions the observed Rust compile time grows about 4–5x and RSS less
than 2x. Startup dominates small inputs; this does not establish a universal
complexity bound. The sparse action store grows with actual nonelided copy uses.

A fixed 512-parenthesis leaf-copy probe starts both implementations at a shared
**1 MiB** stack. `stack.jsonl` reports **match=true** and
**stack_qualification.passed=true**, with exit 0, identical streams and LowIR
at the shared **16 MiB** stack. Its receipt names retained initial and qualified
attempts in `stack-qualifications/`. Qualification judges the protected match,
never the initial diagnostic position. The genuine byte-budget guard is
unchanged; no native exhaustion depth/frame size was calibrated.
`binary-hashes.json` retains the measured tools and matches `final.json`.

(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged. This is a coherent partial implementation handoff
for **pa11 full-stage**, phase **implement**. **PA11 remains incomplete**;

passing identities**. The separate protected 409/411 reporting defect persists.

### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `pa11/README.md`,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and the current stage ledger. Consulted the frozen
implementation directly under `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp`:
`sema/analyzer_conversions.cpp::conditional/conditional_match/converted_conditional/
builtin_conditional`, `sema/analyzer_expressions.cpp`'s cast owner,
`lower/condition_lowering.cpp::conditional_place`,
`lower/discard_lowering.cpp::discard/discard_to_void`,
`lower/expression_lowering.cpp::rvalue/cast/load_glvalue` and
`lower/function_lowering.cpp::load`. Initial raw traces separate class call-result
materialization, implicit converting constructors, operator-arrow calls, ordinary
class designators and conditional base-reference conversion. The last two form
this completed class glvalue group; the other boundaries remain explicit.

- `class_conditionals.rs` checks both standard matching directions using original
  operand TypeIds, categories and cv facts. A direct glvalue binding stays
  distinct from a related-class prvalue/copy match. Selected glvalues check base
  uniqueness and access at the conditional's location, demand only the necessary
  layout, and retain the canonical BasePathId for the selected operand.
- The original conditional ExprId owns two optional paths in a private sparse
  store. Parentheses share its root. Source operands retain their original types,
  categories and conversion records; a later reference binding cannot overwrite
  the conditional's own route. Dense ExprFacts does not grow. The read-only
  lowering view derives offsets solely from these checked paths and ready layouts.
- `conditional_place` selects branch addresses into the existing pointer slot,
  projecting the recorded base after each address, including an explicit zero
  offset. Source evaluation, branch/block creation, pointer reload and store order
  match C++. No type/name search, access decision or layout discovery occurs in
  lowering. Lvalues, xvalues, const/volatile references, nesting and inherited
  access use the same path. A nonzero-offset probe exercises the existing broader
  nonvirtual layout infrastructure without claiming a new assignment boundary.
- Void casts address named class/array objects and references, read ordinary
  scalar objects, evaluate other class designators for effects, and preserve
  volatile reads and assignment/prefix-update treatment. Resolved BindingKind
  distinguishes named variables/parameters from functions. Function designators
  gain no unwanted address demand. No object load replaces a class address.
- Single-argument unbraced void functional casts share the explicit-cast operand
  path when evaluated as a value. The frozen discard owner instead skips a
  discarded void functional cast. This observable rule is preserved, including
  skipped effects; no language correction or new optimization was introduced.
- A semantic ownership regression checks parse-once expression count, parenthesis
  root sharing, retained path/offset, cv/category and unchanged original operand
  types and conversions. It verifies a phase invariant rather than duplicating
  the differential fixture's expected text.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records these responsibilities and Rust owners.
New matching work visits only the operands' inheritance graphs; it never scans
unrelated declarations/functions. Query-local walks die with the query, paths
and sparse use facts with the semantic unit, and branch slots/scratch with one
Function. Only typed LowIR crosses frontend release. No dependency, unsafe code,
FFI, subprocess compiler implementation, textual transport, synthesized syntax,
whole-program polling, environment read, lint waiver, optimizer, register
allocator, backend, fixture, oracle, protected checker or Ralph-state change.
