### C++ owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, the PA11 handout,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and the current ledger. Consulted the frozen C++
implementation directly in `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp`:
`expression_lowering.cpp::temporary_storage/scalar_temporary/extension_path/
bind_extended/extends_temporary/aggregate_extends/bind_converted`,
`object_lowering.cpp::temporary_object/array_temporary/destroy/destroy_local/
destroy_at/local_object`, `aggregate_lowering.cpp::aggregate_item/
aggregate_array_object/drop_member_cleanups/member_made`,
`global_lowering.cpp::static_temporary/name_temporary`,
`statement_lowering.cpp::local_initializer/declared_live/count_returns` and
`lexical_cleanup_lowering.cpp::body_unwinds`.

The earliest failures shared storage/lifetime ownership, before code generation.
The class-call case additionally depends on class-return materialization; it
remains incomplete. The implemented group preserves these production invariants:

- Original ExprIds and the retained reference conversion facts select the
  extension path through parentheses, dot members, array subscripts, reference
  casts and comma operands. Comma evaluation transfers ownership to its right
  operand while evaluating its left operand first. Scalar conversion temporaries
  remain distinct from class objects designated through subobjects.
- `reference_lifetimes.rs` owns per-function temporary paths, active aggregate
  EntityId and binding owner. Local references and nested aggregate reference
  members retain the original declaring EntityId. No syntax is synthesized or
  reparsed, and no semantic resolution or layout is repeated in lowering.
- Runtime scalar bindings and trivial-destructor class/array bindings with
  static duration allocate distinct internal globals, never initializer-frame
  slots. Shared symbol naming consumes the original variable/sequence ABI facts
  for `_ZGR` identities; aggregate-owned array backing names retain their
  separate program counter. Conditional scalar result slots are reused only
  for automatic storage. Startup constructs the backing object before storing
  the reference's address. Dynamic scalar references decline constant-image
  lowering when there is no evaluated image.
- Completed scoped class temporaries with nonthrowing destructors register an
  address-owned `Extended` cleanup. They keep staged exception regions across
  reference stores and remain live through scope, return, break, continue and
  goto transfers. Multiple returns share the existing cleanup continuation.
  The frozen unwinding query's separate reference and aggregate rules remain.
- Aggregate/member-array completion drops only construction-owned actions.
  Retained extended temporaries preserve their owner depths, and chain tails
  that led to retired members are rebuilt for subsequent calls. Earlier emitted
  handlers remain valid. Compaction visits only the affected suffix, matching
  the C++ owner; it does not rescan an unrelated live prefix.
- Explicit one-dimensional class list-array temporaries initialize class
  elements through aggregate construction rather than scalar object stores,
  reuse completed-prefix counters and preserve reverse destruction and the
  existing eight-element destruction boundary. Nested/omitted temporary-array
  initialization still has the separate region/counting boundary below.
- A driver-owned regression parses two reference-only/mixed-member programs,
  then a lower-owned assertion checks distinct owners, completed-member transfer,
  path/context restoration, lexical cleanup retirement and unchanged original
  expression count. Test-only orchestration respects phase dependencies.

All temporary query state, cleanup chains and scratch end with one Function.

## Qualified type names and class completion checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `eb87974fd3d6740ba3a17a6e2f794d1bfa84a2b1`
(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged.
This is a coherent partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**,
phase **implement**. **PA11 remains incomplete**; Ralph owns acceptance,
audits and stage advancement. The preceding goal turn was **progress**,
evidenced by its committed inherited constructor forwarding group and four

passing IDs**. The separate protected reporting discrepancy is recorded below.

### C++ owners, typed flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, the PA11 handout,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen C++ source
in `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp` directly:
`sema/analyzer_types.cpp::named_type/name_prefix/scope_of_binding`,
`analyzer_classes.cpp::class_specifier/class_body/new_class`,
`analyzer_expressions.cpp::member_qualifier`, and
`lookup.cpp::merge/search_inline_set/namespace_members/qualified`.
The initial ADL-named failures already reach the separate nonelided-copy
boundary; their candidate lookup is not the first divergence. The implemented
failures instead share the type-qualifier/class-head owners above.

- `lookup.rs` converts a type or BindingId to its canonical ScopeId. A
  decltype name component uses the original operand ExprId and existing
  unevaluated analysis; its type must be a class or enumeration. References
  and pointers remain distinct types and are rejected as qualifiers. An
  incomplete class has a distinct diagnostic from a scalar or non-type name.
  The same binding conversion serves explicit member qualifiers. A class
  currently being defined remains searchable through its published scope.
- `LookupResult` retains a query-local qualifier ScopeId and an ambiguity
  fact alongside the representative BindingId. Nested operand analysis cannot
  overwrite the outer named-type qualifier. Diagnostic context is restored
  after the operand's lookup; ambiguous nested prefixes retain their own
  diagnostic and location. These are temporary typed query results, with no
  new graph-wide cache, invalidation epoch or dense expression payload.
- `declarators.rs` consumes that result once to distinguish a qualified
  injected-class-name naming its constructor from a type. A typename-specifier,
  elaborated type and an intermediate nested-name prefix retain the oracle's
  different rules. Aliases of the class nominate its canonical identity;
  inherited injected names retain the representative's declaring scope.
- `declarations.rs` finds a qualified class head's previous declaration through
  the existing namespace/inline/using and inherited class indexes. It completes
  that EntityId, preserves the original home for ABI identity, and gives the
  body the nominated prefix's lookup/print context. Enum qualifiers and aliases
  that name no class declaration preserve the C++ diagnostic precedence.
- Namespace merges retain ambiguity rather than throwing before their caller
  can consume the result. Ordinary name uses still diagnose it. The frozen
  class-head owner consumes the representative without testing that flag;
  the Rust class-head path preserves this behavior. Inline members are visited
  in the original oldest-first order after translating the C++ prepended list
  and LIFO frontier; this matters when a class head consumes a representative.
- Three ownership regressions prove unchanged parsed expression count, original
  call facts and resolved bindings, no evaluated call demand or class layout
  demand from a decltype qualifier, one canonical class across an inline forward
  declaration and completion, original versus nominated scope ownership, and
  the representative chosen from an ambiguous inline set.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the translated responsibilities. The
implementation parses once, uses existing interned TypeIds/EntityIds and compact
BindingIds/ScopeIds, and leaves recorded semantic facts for existing lowering.
No new lowering pass, text transport, whole-program search, speculative body
or layout demand, or persistent lookup result cache was introduced. Function
IR/backend ownership, optimizer, register allocator and code generator are
unchanged. No unsafe, FFI, compiler fallback, dependency, environment read,
lint waiver, fixture, reference, oracle, checker, policy or Ralph-state change
was made. This group covers ordinary non-template PA11 names; dependent
qualifiers and specialization completion retain their later-stage boundary.

## Inherited constructor forwarding checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `ceaf3e6ebbde02dd58bbd40b7c63812629ca3b3a`
(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged.
This is a coherent partial handoff for **pa11 full-stage**, phase **implement**.
**PA11 remains incomplete**; Ralph owns acceptance, audits and advancement.
The preceding goal turn was **progress**, evidenced by the committed aggregate
subobject group and its five fixed-manifest gains. This turn is **progress**:
**369 to 373 passing**, **40 to 36 failing**, on the same **409** fixed case
identities, with **zero lost passing IDs**.

### Owners, typed flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, the PA11 handout,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen C++ owners
in `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp` directly:
`sema/analyzer.cpp::using_declaration`,
`analyzer_construction.cpp::declare_inherited_constructors/
record_inherited_constructor/check_inheriting_access/
planned_inheriting_constructor/plan_nothrow/constructor_plan`,
`analyzer_copy.cpp::constructor_candidates`,
`lower/object_lowering.cpp::construct_base/forward_parameters`,
`function_lowering.cpp::lower_definition`, and
`symbol_lowering.cpp::function_encoding`.

The four failures shared their earliest semantic divergence: a constructor
using-declaration imported the base type, but declared no constructor
candidates for the derived class. Initialization then fell into the unsupported
base copy-plan path. The group translates ordinary non-template inheritance:

- `sema/inherited_constructors.rs` owns private sparse canonical class-to-base
  and function-to-source-BindingId maps. Constructor using-declarations introduce
  no ordinary name; alias naming components retain their original syntax.
  Signatures publish at class completion before member-initializer analysis.
  Reverse base-overload enumeration, same-signature hiding, copy/move exclusion,
  trailing-default forms, explicitness, deletion and original binding access
  follow the frozen owner. A private using-declaration does not change access.
- The selected constructor asks for its inherited chain through a bottom-up
  worklist. It reuses canonical ConstructorIds and existing default member,
  array and destructor plans; the forwarded base never asks for an unrelated
  default constructor. A real byte-budget check remains at plan construction.
  Source default ExprIds stay shared. Omitted forwarding defaults contribute
  their throwing facts. Invalid subobject plans diagnose a deleted function at
  the selected initialization, preserving the separate selected-use access check
  and the oracle's default-declaration access path.
- `construction.rs` retains one typed forwarding flag per base plan and shares
  callable/presentation signature construction with ordinary constructors,
  including their original variadic presentation. Synthetic positional parameter
  identities use canonical types and compact EntityIds. The ordinary emitted-
  signature boundary worklist prepares only demanded parameter/layout facts.
  `copy_initialization.rs` rejects a structurally impossible implicit reference
  sequence before demanding destination subobject-copy properties; real
  copy/move construction remains a separate incomplete responsibility.
- `lower/construction.rs` loads positional scalar/reference slots in signature
  order, forwards class parameter slots, evaluates omitted defaults and calls
  the selected base entry. Completed bases/members retain the existing unwind
  cleanup ownership. `function.rs` consumes a read-only positional-prologue
  fact: synthesized object parameters use direct stores, while written class
  parameter declarations retain their existing byte-copy prologues.
- The ownership regression verifies inherited/source signatures and bindings,
  copy-constructor exclusion, original member initializer ExprIds, unchanged
  parsed expression count, canonical positional parameters and undemanded
  constructors/layouts in an unused class. Generated addresses, call arguments,
  parameter slots and cleanup state remain per-function. No text is reparsed.

`source-map.tsv` records these owners. No optimizer, register allocator, native
backend, dependency, environment read, lint waiver, fixture, reference, oracle,
checker, protected policy or Ralph-state change was made. Scope is the PA11
LowIR view and its complete/base entry metadata; this does not certify future
host-object inherited-name encoding, templates or virtual/multiple inheritance.

## Aggregate class subobject initialization checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `147cb09841946bad8585b0c1d43ae8d076bf0448`
(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above are unchanged.
This is a coherent partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**,
phase **implement**. **PA11 remains incomplete**; Ralph owns acceptance,
audits and advancement. The previous goal turn was **progress**, evidenced by
its committed recursive-construction implementation and 13 fixed-manifest gains.
This turn is **progress**: the same 409 fixed identities improve from
**364 to 369 passing**, **45 to 40 failures**, with **zero lost passing IDs**.

### C++ owners, typed flow and responsibility review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, the PA11 handout,
`docs/rust-clone-plan.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen C++ owners
in `/home/vishvananda/work/references/v5rust-cpp` directly:
`sema/analyzer_initialization.cpp::aggregate_element/aggregate_members`,
`analyzer_conversions.cpp::user_conversion/apply_user_conversion`,
`analyzer_construction.cpp`, `analyzer_exceptions.cpp`,
`lower/aggregate_lowering.cpp::aggregate_stores/aggregate_class_item/
materialize_member/aggregate_object/member_made/aggregate_elements/
push_counted/element_made/end_counted/omitted_may_throw/structured_item`,
`cleanup_lowering.cpp::chain_entry/unwind_block_kind/emit_call/region_event/
close_region/destroy_elements/element_temporaries`, and
`object_lowering.cpp::call_special/value_initialize/destroy/destroy_at`.

The earliest divergences shared those aggregate owners: semantic analysis
mistook a nonaggregate class element for brace elision; lowering descended into
its data as if it were an aggregate, skipped empty constructor argument effects,
or rejected nontrivial subobject storage before reaching the final address.
Protected case traces and `clusters.json` retain the first diagnostics and raw
artifact differences. The implemented group includes its unwind invariants:

- `sema/initialization.rs` and `constructor_initialization.rs` distinguish a
  genuine subaggregate from a converting class member. A typed initialization
  context and optional unique overload result preserve the member-conversion
  diagnostic before deletion/access checks on the selected constructor.
  Default arguments and standard source conversions use the existing call path.
- `expression_facts.rs` privately owns sparse original-ExprId-to-selected-
  ConstructorId/ConstructorArguments facts, normalized through expression
  aliases. The source type, source constructor and ordinary argument conversion
  remain distinct. Dense `ExprFacts` does not grow. The ownership regression
  checks source and selected constructor identities, original argument IDs,
  unchanged syntax counts and an unused class's undemanded layout.
- `lower/aggregates.rs` consumes those selections and existing checked prvalue
  elision facts at each final member address. Empty nonaggregate classes still
  call constructors and evaluate arguments. Value/address ordering distinguishes
  named members, constructor members, computed objects and array elements.
- `aggregate_cleanup.rs` owns scoped aggregate construction state in one
  `Function`. Completed subobjects join the unwind chain, then transfer to the
  complete owner. Nested units collapse their child actions; region close and
  completion-address order follow the oracle. Written initializer segments,
  omitted scalar-class value initialization and array elements retain distinct
  call scopes, including nonthrowing calls in an active segment.
- `aggregate_arrays.rs` owns one completed-prefix counter for a member array
  and its flat multidimensional leaf sequence. Nested aggregate elements retain
  their partial member cleanup until counted. Counter stores, reverse cleanup
  loops, and the transition to whole-array ownership match the oracle. Completed
  array cleanup uses the existing eight-element destruction boundary.
- `lifetime.rs` uses `CleanupPlace::{Storage, Subobject, Counted}` and optional
  source-variable identities, shared by its existing interned per-function
  cleanup chains. `lifetime_regions.rs` retains region depth and aggregate
  landing facts. Lowering and semantic throw queries consume the retained
  conversions, including the oracle's default-argument query distinction.

`source-map.tsv` records these owners. Original syntax is parsed once; no
semantic tree or text transport was introduced. Aggregate state, counters,
addresses, chains and pending blocks are released with their function. The
optimizer, register allocator and native backend are unchanged. No unsafe,
FFI, compiler fallback, dependency, environment read, lint waiver, fixture,
reference, oracle, checker, policy or Ralph-state change was made.

## Recursive constructor and direct class argument checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `7e7f3041140661903bd0bb0f5060c0bd02be1498`
(2026-10-15). Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged.
This is a coherent partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**,
phase **implement**. **PA11 remains incomplete**; Ralph owns acceptance,
audits and advancement. The previous goal turn was **progress**, evidenced
by the recursive destructor commit and five fixed-manifest gains. This turn
is **progress**: recursive constructor selection, subobject initialization,
constructor unwind continuations and the directly passed class argument
boundary now reduce the unchanged fixed manifest's failures.

### Owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read the repository guide, clone contract, specification, Rust clone plan,
PA11 handout and current ledger. Consulted the frozen C++ owners directly:
`sema/analyzer_construction.cpp::implicit_default_facts/member_plan/
constructor_plan`, `analyzer_initialization.cpp::check_const_default`,
`analyzer_copy.cpp`, `lower/object_lowering.cpp::initialize_subobjects/
initialize_member/construct_base`, `cleanup_lowering.cpp::construct_elements/
subobject_constructed/destroy_subobject`, `lexical_cleanup_lowering.cpp::
detached_body/body_unwinds/body_terminal/element_exit/flush_deferred`,
`function_lowering.cpp`'s parameter prologues and constructor completion,
`statement_lowering.cpp::return_from_body`, `expression_lowering.cpp::
push_argument/push_converted`, and `symbol_lowering.cpp::passed_directly/
copied_trivially/course_member_quarters/course_class_quarters/course_classes`.
Raw protected traces changed the initial clustering: the two ADL namespace
fixtures already selected the right functions and stop at nonelided class
copy construction. This group follows the earlier constructor divergence.

- `sema/construction.rs` owns `MemberConstruction`: member EntityId, selected
  ConstructorId and original ConstructorArguments, in declaration order.
  Defaulted/implicit plans propagate child triviality/exception facts, const
  eligibility and union variant deletion. Written constructors select omitted
  class/array subobjects, retain written/DMI arguments and validate potential
  subobject destruction. Existing iterative default-plan completion is retained.
- `constructor_initialization.rs` checks class parameter destruction and copy
  elision after constructor selection, on the original argument facts.
  `copy_initialization.rs` retains a private pending/direct/indirect class call
  fact alongside canonical scalar-class copy facts. It is computed once after
  body analysis and read through the lowering API. Indirect ownership remains
  an explicit implementation boundary, with status 86 rather than invalid IR.
- `sema/layout.rs` extends the existing emitted-entity boundary worklist with
  typed constructor roots and owner-keyed actual default/array construction
  requests. Separate EntityId and ConstructorId seen sets follow only selected
  base/member plans and retained function demand edges. This prepares needed
  parameter layouts/copy facts through implicit constructors and default
  arguments without completing unused inline bodies or scanning global classes.
- `lower/construction.rs` consumes those plans for complete/base entries,
  scalar members, class members and array elements. Value initialization preserves
  zero-before-construction and qualified element layout containment facts.
  Scalar and bit-field initializers retain their original dedicated path;
  ordinary offsets are never requested for a bit-field.
- `constructor_cleanup.rs` owns each function's completed subobjects, active
  landing/entry and deferred cleanup chain. Regions open even when following
  actions are nonthrowing, cover the body, end at normal/explicit return and
  unwind in reverse order. Deferred blocks follow the block that opened them.
  `destruction.rs` supplies shared typed subobject actions, small-array address
  recomputation, larger reverse loops and throwing-destructor terminate guards.
- `object_arrays.rs` shares default element construction over typed storage
  and member roots, including partial prefixes, the eight-element boundary,
  multidimensional arrays and detached enclosing constructor continuations.
  `lifetime_scan.rs` detects supported body unwinding; `lifetime.rs` captures
  the constructor entry on each lexical terminal and invalidates only the
  active function's affected unwind memos as a subobject completes.
- `materialization.rs` builds directly passed class prvalue arguments in caller
  `argobj` storage; ordinary/operator/constructor calls share it. Parameter
  prologues use `copyobj` into frame storage and preserve empty-class no-copy
  and functional-cast no-zeroing rules. `class_parameters.rs` memoizes the
  course's four-quarter classification by canonical class, preserving INTEGER
  precedence, SSE/none classes, bit-field allocation units, misalignment and
  long-double MEMORY rules. Definitions, declarations and typed indirect
  signatures publish this through the LowIR builder's parameter-classes API.

The three invariant tests verify retained child/argument identities with no
syntax growth, direct versus indirect scalar-class call facts, and default
array constructor boundary preparation without unused-body completion.
`source-map.tsv` records the owners. All new semantic state belongs to the
unit; all constructor actions, pending blocks and scratch belong to one
Function. The optimizer, register allocator and native backend are unchanged.
No unsafe, FFI, compiler fallback, dependency, environment read, lint waiver,
fixture/reference/oracle/checker/policy change or Ralph state edit was added.
The independent source audit reviews responsibilities/size as well as Clippy.

large/multidimensional arrays, direct class arguments and empty/floating/mixed/
packed/bit-field/character-array parameter metadata.
`runtime.py`, `runtime-validated.log`, `runtime.json` and `runtime.jsonl`
retain **19/19 exact LowIR receipts**, reference backend exits 0 and executable
exits 0, covering lifetime counts/order, constructor body locals, defaulted
and value initialization, array thresholds/rows and direct class arguments.
The frozen backend is only the PA11-authorized evaluator of Rust-produced LowIR.

The next coherent dependency boundary is explicit nontrivial aggregate-list
subobject construction/materialization and its reference-temporary lifetime.
Subsequent groups include nonelided copy/move and indirect/variadic class call
ownership, class-valued returns/conditionals, inherited constructor forwarding,
placement allocation, nested-name/decltype and remaining conversion diagnostics.
`boundary.cpp`, `boundary-validated.log` and `boundary-validated.jsonl` record
an accepted C++ class with a user-provided copy constructor passed by value:
C++ exits 0, Rust explicitly exits **86** at indirect class parameter ownership.
This is a documented unfinished boundary, **not** a matched rejection.
The copied-trivial boundary currently uses the existing scalar-class copy
properties; base/class-subobject copy plans remain due work.

## Recursive nonvirtual destructor checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `f945bcd4667b2e5bc135c0202977ad16216799ac`
(2026-10-15). The stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged. This is a coherent partial implementation handoff for
**pa11 full-stage**, phase **implement**; **PA11 remains incomplete**.
The previous goal turn was **progress**, evidenced by committed text literal
operators and three fixed-manifest gains. This turn is **progress**: typed
recursive destructor properties, implicit callable identities, and destructor
body/subobject cleanup regions now match the frozen C++ implementation.
Ralph owns goal acceptance, final audits and stage advancement.

### Owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `docs/rust-clone-plan.md`,
`pa11/README.md` and this ledger. Consulted the frozen C++ owners directly:
`sema/analyzer_construction.cpp::declare_destructor/implicit_destructor_nothrow/
destructor_deleted/subobject_destructor_usable/check_subobject_destruction`,
`analyzer_bodies.cpp`, `analyzer_expressions.cpp`'s destructor designation,
`lower/cleanup_lowering.cpp::destruction_order/destructor_regions/
destructor_epilogue/destroy_subobject/destroy_elements_guarded/
cleanup_terminate/definition_statements_may_throw`,
`function_lowering.cpp::destructor_body/place`,
`statement_lowering.cpp::return_from_body`,
`object_lowering.cpp::needs_destruction/destroy/subobject_address`, and
`program_lowering.cpp::destructor_does_nothing/demand_destruction` plus
base-entry symbol metadata. Raw traces establish the earliest divergence at
missing class-subobject destruction, including a case named for callable-field
lookup that already selected the correct callable.

- `sema/destruction.rs` owns completed-class `Destruction` facts: triviality,
  deletion and allowed unwinding, including direct bases, recursive class/array
  members, union deletion, references and protected-object access. Declaration
  properties belong to canonical EntityIds. Missing required completed facts
  return an invariant error. Class completion does not emit bodies.
- Implicit destructor bindings remain distinct from declared members and are
  interned only when needed. The typed `Preparation` memo visits only the
  demanded object's containment graph and records boundary dependencies once
  per class. Explicit destructor designation prepares the same dependencies.
  `analyzer.rs` checks subobject usability in the original destructor body scope.
  The invariant regression proves reference exclusion, propagated exception and
  deletion properties, unchanged syntax ownership and no unused output demand.
- `sema/lowering.rs` exposes completed properties, retained subobject layout
  offsets and the canonical destructor EntityId. Defaulted destructor calls are
  evaluation-only precisely when the completed destructor is trivial. No lookup,
  overload resolution, text reconstruction or syntax replay occurs in lowering.
- `lower/destruction.rs` owns one function's reverse member/array/base actions,
  body EH region, explicit-return epilogue, suffix cleanup continuations,
  selector IDs and terminate blocks. Up to eight actions use explicit suffixes;
  above eight a progress slot dispatches into one linear cleanup chain. Arrays
  above eight flattened elements loop; throwing normal array destruction keeps
  its current index so unwinding destroys the remaining elements under terminate
  guards. Multidimensional rows preserve their inherited address forms.
- `function.rs`, `lifetime.rs` and `statements.rs` integrate those actions with
  lexical local cleanup and the outer function guard, preserve local-before-base
  destruction, and flush pending terminate blocks at the same terminated block
  boundaries as C++. No general throwing lexical-destructor support is claimed.
- `symbols.rs` memoizes recursive inertness by EntityId for the translation unit;
  it keeps implicit/demanded emission distinct from inert object roots. External
  base destructor declarations carry the declared entry metadata; source-emitted
  base definitions are emitted once. `mod.rs` consumes implicit callable facts
  without inventing synthetic bodies. All action, block and value scratch is
  released with its function; output demand and inertness memos die with the unit.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records these responsibilities. The optimizer,
register allocator, native backend and their policies are unchanged. No unsafe,
FFI, compiler subprocess implementation, dependency, environment read, lint
waiver, fixture/reference/oracle/checker/policy change or Ralph state edit was
introduced. Source responsibility/size review passes independently of Clippy.

## Scalar and reference text literal operator checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `59f00f2e0f3dfb0ae4824b9931ab6dda4dfef9d3` (2026-10-15). The stage base and
last-audited commit above remain unchanged. This is a coherent partial
implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**, phase **implement**;
**PA11 remains incomplete**. The previous goal turn was **progress**, evidenced
by committed static member/TLS storage and ten retained fixed-manifest gains.
This turn is **progress**: ordinary non-template character and string literal
operators now select and emit exact scalar/reference calls, and user-defined
string literals no longer masquerade as character-array initializers.
Ralph owns goal acceptance, audits and stage advancement.

### Owners, typed data flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `docs/rust-clone-plan.md`,
`pa11/README.md` and this ledger. Read frozen C++
`sema/analyzer_literals.cpp::literal/user_character_literal/user_string_literal/
literal_call`, `analyzer_calls.cpp` and `conversions.cpp`'s best-viable/sequence
ordering, `expression_facts.h`'s literal-call forms,
`string_literals.cpp`, `constant_statements.cpp::user_literal`,
`syntax/parse_token_stream.cpp`, `lower/expression_lowering.cpp::literal/
side_effect_free/place`, `condition_lowering.cpp::literal_truth`,
`global_lowering.cpp::string_literal` and
`cleanup_lowering.cpp`'s selected-call throw query directly. Traces clustered
three fixed failures at missing literal-call facts rather than parsing or
backend generation.

- `syntax/cursor.rs` interns a literal's suffix once while pulling its token.
  The original `ast::Literal` retains `Option<NameId>` instead of an unused
  owning suffix text. String suffix spans retain their existing begin/end
  meaning, including concatenated literals. No later phase reconstructs a
  lookup key from source spelling.
- `sema/literal_calls.rs` owns ordinary literal typing and text-call analysis.
  The selected ordinary unqualified literal-operator lookup uses
  `ValueKey::Special(SpecialName::Literal(NameId))`; it does not invoke ADL.
  The decoded character value/source TypeId, or string element TypeId/unit
  count, survive as typed `TextCall` facts on the original ExprId, alongside
  its chosen direct callable, result category and demand edge. No synthetic
  argument syntax, second parse, or alternate semantic tree is built.
- `procedural_calls.rs` accepts the text arguments as already typed facts,
  uses existing standard conversion sequences and reusable function-call
  scratch, and shares candidate comparison/selection with ordinary calls.
  Deleted, absent, ambiguous and nonviable operators retain exact diagnostics.
  Expected candidate rejection remains a compact result. Scratch is restored
  and cleared on success and error; lookup visits the indexed name only.
- `initialization.rs` distinguishes an ordinary string literal from a call
  written with string-literal syntax. `constants.rs` routes constant evaluation
  through the selected callable: non-constexpr calls reject correctly, while
  required constexpr interpretation remains an explicit status-86 boundary.
  `throw_queries.rs` includes selected literal operators in noexcept queries.
- `lower/literal_calls.rs` consumes the retained callable and decoded operand
  shape. It preserves character initialization conversion and signed immediate
  presentation; string code-unit sharing/read-only storage; address, i32-to-i64
  sign extension of the length, demand and call order; reference-result places;
  discarded calls; and exception-region boundaries. String arrays are shared
  with ordinary literals through the existing per-unit typed data cache.
- `conditions.rs` preserves the frozen `literal_truth` behavior: its test uses
  the literal-call form (character=3, string=0). The inherited literal
  side-effect-free query also remains exact. These behaviors are documented
  and probed rather than replaced with language-level simplifications.
  `throw_scan.rs` consumes the selected function's unwind facts. All builders,
  argument values and cleanup scratch remain function-owned and are released
  after streaming. The optimizer, allocator and native backend are unchanged.

## Static data members and first-use TLS storage checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `2546d571abb85e1453c21c9a06a930ec50224d6a`.
Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged. This is a coherent
partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**; **PA11 remains
incomplete**. The previous goal turn was **progress**, evidenced by the committed
leaf storage lifetime group and its ten retained fixed-manifest gains. This turn
is **progress**: static data member definitions now use their original class
identity and initialization, and dynamically initialized namespace/class TLS
objects have first-use guards, wrappers and guarded leaf finalization.
Ralph owns checkpoint/final audits and advancement.

### Original owners, typed flow and architecture review

Read `AGENTS.md`, `CLONE_CONTRACT.md`, `spec.md`, `docs/rust-clone-plan.md`,
`pa11/README.md` and this ledger. The fixed traces clustered static scalar,
class, aggregate-array and TLS-member failures at the namespace-only symbol
path. Consulted frozen `sema/analyzer_declarations.cpp::declare_variable/
earlier_variable/declarator_target/new_variable`,
`analyzer_expressions.cpp::odr_use`, `semantic_unit.h::note_initializer_constructor`,
`lower/program_lowering.cpp::simple_declaration/define_variable/demand/
emit_demanded/finish`, `symbol_lowering.cpp::variable_symbol/variable_name/
variable_object/unit_local`, `global_lowering.cpp::static_global/define_global/
thread_local_initialization/thread_local_wrapper_symbol/finalizer_for`,
`function_lowering.cpp::lower_thread_local_initializer/lower_thread_local_wrapper`,
`expression_lowering.cpp::variable_place`, `object_lowering.cpp::finalize_global`
and `definition_order.cpp` directly.

- `sema/declarations.rs` and `data_members.rs` use the existing indexed qualified
  lookup for qualified variable declarations, including an inherited static
  member. The definition publishes a new binding of the canonical entity,
  preserving its real class owner, type completion and access context. A
  nonconstant in-class const integral initializer is rejected at its original
  declarator, rather than reaching an incorrect uninitialized definition.
- `sema/construction.rs`, `data_members.rs` and `lowering.rs` retain the original
  in-class initializer and request its static image when an out-of-class
  definition omits an initializer. No syntax is synthesized and no expression
  is retyped. `VariableFacts` exposes that original initializer by value; class
  owner unit locality is separate from declared internal linkage. Program
  storage includes static data members while layout still includes instance
  members only.
- `lower/symbols.rs` uses the namespace/class presentation path and existing
  typed ABI scope/name graph for variables. Symbol caches remain keyed by
  `EntityId`; `_ZTW` names are encoded from ABI nodes. Wrapper binding follows
  variable unit locality, including an unnamed-namespace class. Collision-safe
  guard and initializer symbols use the original `__cppgm_tls_*` family.
- `globals.rs` emits only a variable's defining declarator, reuses its in-class
  initializer and shares scalar/class/array startup and shutdown paths. Imported
  arrays and class objects have untyped declarations, even when complete here.
  TLS definitions do not create ordinary startup steps.
- `thread_local.rs` owns first-use initialization: read the thread's i64 guard
  through its address, branch to the initializer only when zero, execute the
  existing selected scalar/class/array plan, publish one after success, then
  converge on the exact done block. Its function-owned builder/scratch is
  released after streaming. A wrapper calls this initializer then returns the
  thread address. Uses before a static definition retain their address-only
  wrapper; uses of dynamic definitions retain their initializing wrapper.
- `mutations.rs` shares `global_place` between id-expressions and explicit
  member syntax. Explicit receiver effects happen first; reference members
  subsequently load their referent through the same holder path. Lowering does
  no lookup, constructor selection or source reparsing.
- `shutdown.rs` retains an optional guard `SymbolId` with the existing finalizer
  object/callee IDs, array shape and target sizes. Dynamic TLS objects whose
  guard is zero are skipped; initialized objects and arrays are destroyed in
  reverse definition/element order. Guard branches and array loops share the
  original block ordinal sequence. No semantic entity survives its source unit.

`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records these responsibilities and remaining limits.
The optimizer, register allocator and native backend are unchanged. No unsafe,
FFI/compiler fallback, new dependency, environment read, lint waiver, protected
fixture/reference/oracle/checker change, or Ralph state edit was introduced.
A source audit verifies the source/function limits independently of Clippy.

### Remaining groups and next dependency boundary

The next major group is still **recursive class subobject construction and
destruction**, including implicit destructor identities/exception/deletion facts,
base/member plans, constructor-prefix EH regions, destructor body ordering and
aggregate-array cleanup. Remaining related storage boundaries are nontrivial
extended reference temporaries, block-static class lifetimes, throwing destructor
cleanup, and later object-mode unit TLS/host registration. Inherited constructor
selection, class value ABI/copy viability, lookup/ADL, decltype and UDLs remain
separate semantic groups. No complete PA11 claim is made.

A retained independent probe exposes **ordinary global constructor/destructor
first-demand order**: `ordinary-default-before-construction.cpp` and
`ordinary-braced-before-construction.cpp`, driven by `demand-probes.py` /
`demand-probes.jsonl`. An earlier `Check c` with a user destructor, followed by
`V a(1)`, emits the Check destructor before the V constructor in Rust; C++ emits
V's constructor first. Instruction bodies and finalization actions agree, but
raw definition and ABI alias order differ. The same shape occurs with TLS in
`default-before-tls.cpp` and
`guarded-finalization-demand-boundary.runtime.cpp`. All four raw mismatches are
retained and **not counted as exact runtime parity**. This reproduces without
static data members or TLS; it is an existing broader initialization-demand
boundary, not an oracle defect or a permission to reorder output. Its owners
are `program_lowering.cpp::demand/emit_demanded`, `global_lowering.cpp::define_global/
finalizer_for` and `lower/globals.rs` / `symbols.rs` / `mod.rs`. The successful
shutdown runtime uses an ordinary out-of-class destructor definition and proves
first-use guard semantics independently of that unsupported ordering shape.

## Completed leaf-object storage lifetime checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `aec54d96059c5f5fc5eaa7fab3797dcefa079689`.
Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged. This is a coherent
partial implementation handoff for **pa11 full-stage**; **PA11 remains
incomplete**. The previous goal turn was **progress**, evidenced by the
committed prvalue group and its retained fixed-manifest gains. This turn is
**progress**: completed automatic and namespace leaf objects now have their
exact storage lifetime, with default array construction and reverse destruction,
lexical transfers, shared returns and nonthrowing-function exception boundaries.
Ralph owns acceptance, audits and advancement.

### Completed group and original owners

Read the repository instructions, clone contract, specification, conversion
plan, PA11 handout and current stage ledger. Protected traces clustered the
largest lowering group at `class storage destruction lowering`; recursive
semantic construction/destruction failures are an earlier, distinct boundary.
Read frozen `sema/analyzer_construction.cpp::check_destruction`,
`analyzer_declarations.cpp`, `lower/object_lowering.cpp::local_object/destroy/
destroy_local/destroy_at/destroy_cleanups/leave_scope/construct_array/
construct_global/finalize_global`, `cleanup_lowering.cpp::begin_full_expression/
begin_initializer/begin_construction/begin_call/open_region/emit_call/
close_region/chain_entry/unwind_entry/return_entry/construct_elements/
destroy_array_loop/definition_statements_may_throw/place_terminate_landing`,
`statement_lowering.cpp::returned_value/count_returns/declared_live/shared_return/
goto_statement/substatement`, `lexical_cleanup_lowering.cpp`,
`tracked_lifetime_lowering.cpp`, `function_lowering.cpp`,
`program_lowering.cpp::destructor_does_nothing/root_destructor/terminate_helper/
terminate_function/emit_demanded/finish`, `global_lowering.cpp::finalizer_for/
finalizer_function`, `lowir/builder.cpp`, `text_writer.cpp` and
`syntax/ast_statements.h::LiveThen/LiveElse` directly. Ordinary constant `if`
branches are both visited; only the C++ `if constexpr` reading discards one.

- `lower/lifetime.rs` owns completed-object cleanup actions and their three
  cached continuations: ordinary unwinding, the separate large live-set chain,
  and shared returns. Registration follows successful construction. Scope ends,
  return, break, continue and forward/backward goto destroy the required suffix
  in reverse order. Inert single-object destructors remain defined/object-rooted
  without calls; arrays still call their nontrivial inert element destructors.
- `lifetime_scan.rs` performs one lazy original-body walk. Compact persistent
  `LiveId` parent chains record label live sets, and canonical `EntityId` maps
  retain return counts and completed cleanup depths. Goto follows the target's
  chain and indexed local depths. Shared returns require the same innermost
  live object, retain the value in the continuation slot before ending the
  expression region, and reuse the exact destruction suffix.
- `lifetime_regions.rs` and `throw_scan.rs` consume selected calls, constructors,
  defaults, value categories and original syntax. They preserve call-result
  spills, late region placement before argument evaluation, branch-local region
  ends, handler placement and result preservation. Nonthrowing definitions whose
  code may throw install the exact pre-prologue guard; their landing preserves
  the exception, calls the terminate helper and emits the reference zero return.
  Unwinding chains under that guard perform the matching terminate action.
  Expression-query and pending cleanup buffers retain their capacity for reuse
  within the function; both are released with that function.
- `materialization.rs` distinguishes final named storage from temporaries and
  constructor members. Selected/elided class prvalues can construct a final leaf
  object whose declaration owns destruction. Nonempty functional aggregate
  lists for automatic objects retain their original list ID and named destination
  path (including repeated field addresses). The typed `NamedStorage` enum keeps
  namespace objects on their computed-address aggregate helper path. Nontrivial
  temporary tracking and constructor-member partial-cleanup guards remain at
  their own boundaries.
- `object_arrays.rs` consumes the existing canonical element constructor plan.
  Default arrays flatten their construction sequence, unroll through eight
  elements and use an index loop beyond eight. Throwing constructor/default
  argument paths destroy only the successfully constructed prefix. Named arrays
  destroy last to first, preserving repeated addresses, row offsets and the
  separate destruction loop threshold. Namespace arrays share these typed plans.
- `shutdown.rs` builds one `role=fini` body in reverse declaration/unit order.
  Program-owned finalizer records contain only LowIR `SymbolId`s, array shape
  and target element size; no front-end graph or type ID survives its unit.
  `exception_helpers.rs` similarly owns typed runtime helper declarations and
  the single demanded noinline terminate helper. Runtime declarations publish
  immediately ahead of ordinary pending declarations; definition/helper demand
  order and synthetic-name collisions remain exact.
- `lowir/builder.rs::Position/insert_marker` retain stable block/instruction
  identities while adding a late EH marker. Reverse position search visits only
  the newly emitted argument suffix. A new unit test verifies value-definition
  identity, target identity and valid final LowIR after insertion.
- Analysis records storage-destruction boundary edges separately from output
  demand. `sema/layout.rs` follows those edges in the existing deduplicated
  entity worklist to prepare reached destructor bodies and their local storage.
  This fixes a destructor body containing another local class object without
  emitting an unused body or adding early destructor emission roots. Block-static
  scalar initialization closes its region before publishing its ready flag.

Architecture review: one original syntax owner, canonical selected constructor
and destructor identities, function-owned cleanup/CFG scratch, compact persistent
live-set identities, sparse typed dependency edges and finalizer records without
front-end ownership. Lowering does no lookup, constructor reselection, syntax
synthesis or text reparsing. Optimizer, register allocator and native backend
sources are unchanged. No unsafe, FFI/compiler fallback, dependency, environment
read, lint suppression or protected-input/policy change was added. Updated
`docs/port/source-map.tsv` records each translated responsibility and its limits.

## Class prvalue construction and materialization checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `e7e814f81188aa96bcf6562c48f774ae3c57044c`.
Stage base and last-audited commit above remain unchanged. This is a coherent
partial implementation handoff; **PA11 remains incomplete**. The previous goal
turn was **progress**, evidenced by committed startup work and nine retained
manifest gains. This turn is **progress**: class expression initialization now
uses the original typed selection throughout analysis, materialization and
exception queries. Ralph owns acceptance, audits and advancement.

### Completed group and original owners

Read AGENTS.md, CLONE_CONTRACT.md, spec.md, the PA11 handout, the conversion plan
and current ledger. Raw protected traces distinguished missing class functional
casts from ADL failures: most of the friend/ADL cases in this group stopped in
`elide_class_copy` or nonscalar value initialization before lookup. Lifetime
and recursive-copy cases remain separate named dependencies.

Consulted frozen `sema/analyzer_expressions.cpp::functional_cast/
explicit_conversion`, `analyzer_construction.cpp::class_prvalue/
parenthesized_aggregate/initialize_class/value_initialize`,
`analyzer_calls.cpp::call/argument_dependent`, `analyzer_initialization.cpp`,
`analyzer_exceptions.cpp::may_throw`, `constant_construction.cpp`,
`expression_facts.h`, `lower/object_lowering.cpp::materialize_prvalue/
temporary_object/construct/value_initialize/subobject_address`,
`expression_lowering.cpp::bind_reference/temporary_storage`,
`aggregate_lowering.cpp::helper_shape/aggregate_helper_call`,
`discard_lowering.cpp`, `constant_lowering.cpp::constant_item/
constant_placements/constant_data`, `global_lowering.cpp::static_global` and
`lowir/local_names.cpp` directly.

- `sema/class_casts.rs` owns functional and explicit class cast initialization.
  It preserves class cv-qualifiers, validates completeness, and reuses the
  constructor initialization-use selector for written/default argument
  conversions, explicit/deleted/access checks and copy-elision selection.
  `ClassInitialization` is a compact enum on the original expression:
  `Constructed(ConstructorArguments)`, `Aggregate(original List<ExprId>)`, or
  `Elided(original ExprId)`. The selected ConstructorId remains independent of
  the use's conversions. No cloned tree or synthesized aggregate list exists.
- Parenthesized aggregate arguments use the original list and its consumed
  cursor. A scoped ExprId set separates their allowed outer narrowing from
  nested braced narrowing, and is restored on success/error. Same-class sources
  and braced operands retain checked copy selection. User conversion ranking
  and recursive copy/move remain explicit missing owners.
- `lower/materialization.rs` consumes the enum and ConstructorId directly:
  selected constructors, aggregate initialization or an elided source initialize
  the final destination. Automatic objects, argument/local reference bindings,
  discarded temporaries, temporary-member receivers and constructor member
  expressions share this path. Slots, ordinal naming and LowIR scratch belong
  to one function; construction identities/demand remain unit-owned.
- Ordinary braced local declarations keep their original initialization path
  and address order. Constructor members compute `this`/field address before
  materializing their original expression. Persistent reference storage and
  nontrivial destructor cleanup still report their named missing owner rather
  than constructing an object with an incorrect lifetime.
- `aggregate_helpers.rs` ports the scalar/reference temporary helper shape,
  leading parameter count and trailing zero stores, preserving count/cv helper
  identity and exact demand order. Arrays, class members, bit-fields and unions
  retain their final-address path. The frozen helper body uses member cv for
  stores; its top-level volatile identity alone does not add volatile stores.
  Empty aggregate prvalues use the existing exact contiguous/volatile/union
  zeroing policy and a memoized per-class value-zero property.
- `sema/static_objects.rs` distinguishes evaluated value zeros from omitted
  aggregate members: arithmetic and bit units are scalar zero items, pointers
  and padding are zero bytes. Trivial aggregate value initialization and
  checked elided wrappers reuse canonical types and ready layouts. Arrays and
  nested plain aggregates recurse through their owning image query; shared or
  split bit units still decline static placement as the C++ does. General
  constexpr object evaluation remains a later dependency.
- `throw_queries.rs` consumes the same enum for constructor/default argument
  exception checks and functional-call argument traversal. Aggregate/elided
  expressions retain their original children and destructor exception checks;
  no braced-only recovery or fabricated no-throw fact is used.
- `call_lookup.rs` preserves the C++ absence distinction: a qualified operator
  or literal-function callee reports `undeclared function name`; an absent
  qualified identifier retains ordinary lookup's `undeclared name`.

Architecture review: one original syntax store, canonical TypeId/EntityId/
ConstructorId facts, one dense typed initialization-use enum, sparse scoped
argument context, cached class storage properties and typed static images.
Lowering performs no lookup, constructor redecision, syntax synthesis or text
round trip. Backend/optimizer/register allocator sources are unchanged. No
unsafe, FFI, compiler fallback, new dependency, environment read, lint exception
or protected-file change was introduced. `docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the
translated responsibilities. Function dispatch stays within the companion
source limits by separating class materialization from ordinary designation.

## Declaration-ordered startup initialization checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `c40b657f3dda02962acf30d00cee1358f5b92987`.
The stage base and last-audited commit above are unchanged. This is a coherent
partial implementation handoff; **PA11 remains incomplete**. The previous goal
turn was **progress**, evidenced by committed intrinsic/terminal-call work and
three retained-manifest gains. This turn is **progress**: nine fixed failures
are removed, with every entry passing identity retained. Ralph owns acceptance,
audits and stage advancement; no Ralph state or goal was created or replaced.

### Completed group and original owners

Read the repository guide, clone contract, specification, conversion plan, PA11
handout and current ledger. Clustered the entry 109 failures by their earliest
divergent owner using protected raw traces. Packed bit-field global cases first
failed at static image selection, while their split runtime access operations
were already translated. The frozen C++ deliberately declines constant images
for shared/split units and initializes them at startup; emitting packed data
instead would violate its observable initialization behavior.

Consulted frozen `lower/global_lowering.cpp::static_global/static_object/
static_element/static_aggregate/memberwise_constructor/define_global/
initializer_function`, `function_lowering.cpp::lower_dynamic_initializer/
begin_unit/finish_initializer`, `object_lowering.cpp::construct_global/
construct_array`, `aggregate_lowering.cpp::aggregate_class_item/aggregate_array/
element_step/path_address`, `statement_lowering.cpp::initialize_storage`,
`constant_lowering.cpp::demand_constructions`, `lowir/builder.{h,cpp}`,
`sema/analyzer_initialization.cpp::aggregate_element`,
`analyzer_construction.cpp::value_initialize`,
`analyzer_expressions.cpp::odr_use` and
`semantic_unit.h::note_initializer_constructor` directly.

- `lower/globals.rs` owns a typed static/dynamic outcome. Missing constant
  scalar, pointer or reference images, selected ordinary constructor uses,
  and dynamic aggregate images receive the exact scalar zero or sized zero
  backing and append required startup work at their declaration. Constant
  images preserve their existing path. Dynamic storage is writable; genuine
  constant storage retains the readonly rule. Dynamic TLS remains separate.
- `lower/startup.rs` owns one program-wide startup body. It appends original
  initializer actions immediately, preserving declaration/unit order and
  first-demand order. `lowir/builder.rs::SuspendedFunction` moves the owned
  signature/function/body, insertion block and unrendered slot-name requests
  out of the program borrow between initializers. Compact LowIR IDs, generated
  temporary/slot ordinals, block allocation and termination state survive;
  neither syntax nor semantic references cross the unit boundary. The driver
  finalizes this body once and renders it after streamed definitions.
- Shared `Function::new` creates ordinary or synthetic function scratch.
  Synthetic startup has no fabricated source EntityId or result TypeId;
  source return in that context is a structured invariant failure. Ordinary
  per-function backend/body lifetimes and byte-budget checks remain intact.
  Initializer scratch ends with each full-expression. Temporary naming retains
  its original variable owner and restores it on success/error.
- `lower/aggregates.rs` consumes global storage roots without the extra local
  object's leading address. Member values precede their recomputed addresses;
  array elements retain their required address order. Class element paths emit
  the original multiply/index projection instead of pre-folding an offset.
  Namespace arrays do not substitute automatic-array aggregate helpers.
  Shared, narrow and split bit-field units reuse the original runtime masking,
  signedness, merge and piece order, including volatile memory facts.
- Nonaggregate braced class subobjects consume the selected ConstructorId on
  the original expression and its original argument list. Omitted elements
  consume their already completed default plan. Value initialization, selected
  parameter/default conversions, constructor entry aliases and demand order
  reuse `construction.rs`; lowering performs no overload or lookup redecision.
- `sema/static_arrays.rs` retains distinct complete images and failed prefixes.
  The latter expose only typed address demands made before a later dynamic
  member, so literal/function/reference symbols appear at the same point as
  the C++ constant attempt. The strict memberwise-constructor static rule
  checks an empty written body, no bases, the original parameter/member types
  and one same-position parenthesized parameter action per member. Different
  types, order, braced actions and body effects retain dynamic initialization.
- A private sparse initializer-constructor store records nontrivial selected
  uses during original variable analysis, excluding unevaluated/body uses and
  consecutive duplicate edges. A scoped initialization owner is restored on
  both success and error. Constant initialization still demands these bodies
  in the original reverse-head order. Constructor calls remain separate from
  static-image selection and demand-driven definition emission.
- Persistent reference temporaries remain a named dependency. Startup records
  that namespace-object bindings need persistent storage and returns status
  86 for that missing owner, instead of writing a reference to the startup
  function's automatic frame. Ordinary argument temporaries retain their
  existing full-expression path. No status 86 is accepted as a C++ rejection.

Architecture review: original AST and dense original-expression facts, canonical
TypeIds/EntityIds/ConstructorIds, sparse typed initialization edges and one owned
LowIR startup body. No second tree, token replay, textual phase transport,
whole-program polling, emitted-name reconstruction, fixture recognition,
unsafe/FFI, new dependency, environment read or compiler fallback. No lint
exception was added. Optimizer, register allocator and native encoder sources
are unchanged. `docs/port/source-map.tsv` records these original responsibilities.

Each input has one packed class with a 3-bit signed member, a 64-bit member
spanning nine bytes, and a scalar tail; 100/400/1,600 separately declared globals
append its required startup stores. All measured output hashes agree. Runtime
is observed independently; no new optimization or PA35 speed target is imposed.

| Globals | Source bytes | Rust mean ms / peak RSS KiB | C++ mean ms / peak RSS KiB |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 100 | 3689 | 24.28 / 8748 | 8.93 / 8184 |
| 400 | 14789 | 29.54 / 8756 | 14.29 / 10228 |
| 1600 | 59789 | 67.37 / 15804 | 41.07 / 17596 |

In this supported range, 16x as many globals take about 2.8x Rust wall time
and 1.8x peak RSS; process startup dominates the smaller inputs. These
observations do not prove a universal complexity bound. Startup body storage
necessarily grows with its required operations; ordinary function/backend
scratch remains scoped to each emitted definition.

## Floating intrinsic and terminal-call boundary checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `eb9b18e362f73a12f8d3e5cc3e32c08f9eefc8e1`.
The stage base and last-audited commit above are unchanged. This is a coherent
partial implementation handoff; **PA11 remains incomplete**. The previous goal
turn was **progress**, evidenced by the committed explicit destructor group,
four fixed gains and retained case identities. This turn is **progress**:
three additional fixed failures are removed with every entry passing identity
retained. Ralph owns checkpoint acceptance, audits and stage advancement.

### Completed group and original owners

Read AGENTS.md, CLONE_CONTRACT.md, spec.md, the conversion plan, PA11 handout
and current ledger. Inspected the protected 112-failure set by earliest
owner. The lifecycle cluster still crosses recursive construction/destruction
plans and exception cleanup scheduling. The independent intrinsic cluster
first diverged at builtin declaration/operation analysis, before lowering.
Consulted frozen `lex/gnu_builtins.{h,cpp}`, `sema/analyzer_builtins.cpp`,
`analyzer_calls.cpp`, `analyzer_expressions.cpp::id/typed_value`,
`analyzer_exceptions.cpp::may_throw`, `analyzer_declarations.cpp`'s ordinary
and special definition/attribute paths, `analyzer_classes.cpp::attribute_alignment`,
`constant_expressions.cpp::intrinsic_value/entity_place`,
`semantics_dump.cpp::call`, `lower/intrinsic_lowering.cpp::intrinsic/
classify_nan/classify_infinite/classify_finite/classify_normal/sign_bit/quiet_bit`,
`expression_lowering.cpp::call` and `symbol_lowering.cpp`.

- `sema/floating_intrinsics.rs` owns a typed `FloatingIntrinsic` enum for
  eight classifications (`isfinite`, `isinf`, `isnan`, `isnormal`,
  `fpclassify`, `signbit`, `isinf_sign`, `issignaling`) and six quiet
  comparisons. Interned reserved names feed the existing lazy builtin
  registry. One global function identity with the original `int(...)`
  placeholder type survives repeated and deferred lookup; ordinary source
  declarations still block builtin creation. `__builtin_unreachable` has
  its original ordinary `void()` function identity and arity checks.
- Analysis checks arity before typing, types operands in original order,
  retains their source cv/category and conversion carriers, applies the
  floating common type to quiet comparisons, and records only the selected
  intrinsic on the original dense ExprId facts. No ordinary CallTarget or
  synthetic callee expression is created. Non-call singleton intrinsic
  designation rejects with the original diagnostic. Qualified/parenthesized
  calls, source shadowing, malformed operands and code conversions match.
- Semantic dumps retain the original cast-expression view for intrinsic
  calls lacking a callee designation. `noexcept` follows the C++ intrinsic
  callee-only traversal, including its observable omission of operand
  throwing effects. This is translated behavior, not a corrected oracle.
- The existing constant-call owner now dispatches retained intrinsic facts
  before attempting to evaluate a callee. Supported typed scalar images
  supply target floating bits for classification and quiet comparison;
  signed zeros compare equal, negative magnitudes reverse order, and
  fpclassify evaluates only the selected code and converts it to target int.
  Parameters/non-constexpr variables return structured nonconstant results,
  so optional constant queries return false and required parameter-bound
  queries retain the original diagnostic. General floating arithmetic and
  constexpr-object interpretation remain explicit dependent work below.
- `lower/floating_intrinsics.rs` evaluates floating operands exactly once
  and expands directly into typed LowIR. It preserves each comparison,
  truncation, binary operation and code-evaluation order, explicit target
  infinity/minimum-normal bits, and the x87 sign word's original byte-index
  sequence. Sign/quiet-bit extraction uses the original generated slot name
  and storage width. The five classification codes use a fixed stack array;
  no per-call heap buffer, eager declaration or helper function is introduced.
  Unreachable calls emit the existing block terminator without demanding a
  declaration or emitting a function call; surrounding expression/control
  behavior remains the original lowering's.
- FunctionProperties owns the nondependent `no_inline` request. Ordinary
  declarations join leading and declarator attributes; ordinary/special
  definitions retain the original leading-only attribute rule. Redeclarations
  preserve the request, while a separate block-scope function identity keeps
  its own request without changing the namespace function it denotes.
  Lowering consumes this fact for ordinary functions and both ctor/dtor ABI
  entries. The C++ main metadata early return remains exact.

Architecture review: original source AST, compact TypeId/BindingId/EntityId
relationships and original ExprId facts; no second semantic tree, replayed
syntax, textual phase transport, emitted-name interpretation, or global retry.
Builtin lookup visits only the reserved name family and a fixed-size registry;
constant evaluation and lowering consume typed facts. Temporary numbering,
slots, classification code storage and backend scratch are function-owned.
No new optimization, native encoder/allocator change, unsafe, FFI, dependency,
environment read, compiler subprocess fallback or lint exemption. No frozen
fixture, reference, oracle source, protected checker/policy or Ralph state was
edited. `docs/port/source-map.tsv` records every translated responsibility.

### Resource and protected stack evidence

`measure.py`, `measure.log`, `resources.json`, `scaling.jsonl` and
`binary-hashes.json` retain generated supported sources, every raw observation,
exact protected receipts, and both compiler binary identities. Measurement
ran after all validation jobs finished, with A/A calibration followed by two
paired ABBA blocks. Each function classifies an x87 operand with five codes
and tests its signed infinity. Every measured output hash agrees across Rust
and C++. These are source compile costs; no generated-code optimization or
equal-speed requirement is claimed.

| Functions | Rust mean ms | Rust peak RSS KiB | C++ mean ms | C++ peak RSS KiB |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 50 | 414.43 | 8756 | 10.38 | 8160 |
| 200 | 1655.45 | 8024 | 30.53 | 9448 |
| 800 | 6312.32 | 13672 | 75.56 | 12220 |

Rust wall time scales approximately with function count in this supported
probe, but the constant cost is substantial. Repeated minimum-normal x87
literal formatting in the existing LowIR serializer is a likely contributor:
its exact decimal scanner computes the target value for each occurrence.
This is a source-inspection hypothesis, not a completed profiling attribution.
Serializer measurement/caching is follow-up work, with byte-identical spelling
and bounded ownership required; no speed target is silently waived or new
optimization imposed.

starts both tools with a 1 MiB stack through the protected adapter.
`stack.jsonl` records **match=true**, **stack_qualification.passed=true**:
both exit 0 with identical streams/counters/LowIR at 16 MiB. Raw attempts
remain under `stack-qualifications/cff7d0de52744e5994acc04cdf3273f2`.

calibrated, and the genuine byte-budget guards are unchanged.

## Explicit destructor designation and invocation checkpoint

Implementation base HEAD: `4095e85f7d7f7c5c5e4c250f238b3db9d3deb626`.
The stage base and last-audited commit above are unchanged. This is a coherent
partial implementation handoff; **PA11 remains incomplete**. The previous goal
turn was **progress**, evidenced by the committed named local-class group and
its six fixed gains. This turn is **progress**: four additional fixed failures
are removed, with every entry passing identity retained. Ralph owns acceptance,
audits and stage advancement.

### Completed group and original owners

Read AGENTS.md, CLONE_CONTRACT.md, spec.md, the conversion plan, PA11 handout
and current ledger. Clustered the protected failures by their first divergent
owner. The scalar/enum destructor fixtures failed at the class-only member
check; class destructor fixtures reached unsupported special-name designation.
Consulted frozen `sema/analyzer_expressions.cpp::member_access/destructor_access/
member_qualifier/check_resolved/convert`, `analyzer_calls.cpp`'s bound destructor
branch, `analyzer_construction.cpp::declare_destructor/implicit_destructor_nothrow`,
`analyzer_declarations.cpp`'s special declarations and inline policy,
`analyzer_exceptions.cpp::may_throw`, `expression_facts.h`,
`semantics_dump.cpp::call/node/trailing`,
`lower/expression_lowering.cpp::call`,
`program_lowering.cpp::define_function/emit_definition/demand/destructor_does_nothing`,
`symbol_lowering.cpp` and `definition_order.cpp`.

- `sema/destructor_expressions.rs` handles destructor names before ordinary
  class-only member access. It retains the original object TypeId, category,
  cv and ExprId; looks up the terminal type in the completed naming class then
  the expression context; checks canonical unqualified type agreement; and
  checks deletion and special-member access at the original member location.
  Qualified base destruction retains its typed BasePathId for lowering.
- `DestructorTarget::Scalar` and `Class(BindingId)` distinguish the original
  designation and call facts. Arguments are analyzed before the zero-arity
  diagnostic. Class destructor calls bypass ordinary implicit-object overload
  ranking, as C++ requires for const/volatile objects, while retaining direct
  origin demand and the selected destructor's exception specification. Scalar
  pseudo-calls retain no synthetic function target; noexcept visits their
  original object expression. Bound designations still reject as values or
  function-pointer arguments through the existing owning conversion/resolution
  checks.
- A completed supported class with scalar subobjects publishes an implicit
  trivial destructor only on actual designation demand. The class owns one
  memoized BindingId; its private graph publication API gives it public access
  and the canonical member identity. It remains separate from user-declared
  destructor facts and indexes, preserving copy/move suppression rules.
  Nontrivial subobject completion stays an explicit unsupported boundary.
  The semantic dump renders the implicit definition from these typed facts.
- Lowering evaluates a pseudo-call's arrow operand or dot address exactly once,
  preserving volatile pointer reads and nested call effects, and emits no
  function call/declaration for scalar or trivial class destruction. Other
  explicit calls consume the retained receiver route and direct destructor.
- `FunctionName::Destructor` supplies the canonical class label and D1/D2 ABI
  terminals. Source-defined out-of-class destructors emit a typed
  `DestructorBase(EntityId)` body before the complete body; demand-only inline
  destructors emit their complete body with a D2 object alias. Internal emitted
  destructors retain object_root without giving that definition flag to an
  unresolved declaration. Inline preference joins declarations, so an earlier
  inline declaration also governs an out-of-class body and its demand policy.
- Empty complete destructor definitions retain their serialized span until the
  current unit ends, in original completion order. The body and function scratch
  are released immediately. Source-defined base entries remain in their source
  position; held spans release before the next unit begins. Independent probes
  cover both this ordering and external/internal identity across two units.

Architecture review: one source AST and dense original ExprId facts; compact
BindingId/EntityId/TypeId/BasePathId relationships; no second semantic tree,
reparsing, emitted-name decoding, whole-program retry or new optimization.
Implicit identities are class-owned and queried once; ordinary member and base
lookup visits the original owner sets. Function bodies, temporary numbering and
backend scratch remain per-function. Only serialized output spans survive
completion. Existing genuine byte-budget stack guards are unchanged. No unsafe,
FFI, dependency, environment access, compiler subprocess fallback or lint
suppression was introduced. Optimizer/native encoding/allocator code is
unchanged. No fixture, reference, oracle, protected policy or Ralph state was
edited. `docs/port/source-map.tsv` records the C++/Rust responsibilities.

### Resource and protected stack evidence

`measure.py`, `measure-isolated-final.log`, `resources.json`, `scaling.jsonl`
and `binary-hashes.json` retain supported generated inputs, exact protected
receipts, binary identities, A/A calibration and paired ABBA measurements.
The final measurement ran after validation jobs completed. Each function
explicitly destroys a const class pointer and a volatile scalar object; its
class destructor updates one global. Every measured output hash agrees across
Rust and C++. These are compile-cost observations; no new generated-code
optimization or equal-speed requirement is claimed.

| Functions | Rust mean ms | Rust peak RSS KiB | C++ mean ms | C++ peak RSS KiB |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 50 | 19.80 | 8756 | 7.21 | 8176 |
| 200 | 23.66 | 8724 | 7.67 | 8816 |
| 800 | 49.24 | 10876 | 18.01 | 10216 |

The 512-level expression in a pseudo-destructor's object operand starts both
compilers with a 1 MiB stack through the protected adapter. `stack.jsonl`
records **match=true**, **stack_qualification.passed=true** and both exit 0
with identical streams/counters/LowIR at 16 MiB. Raw attempts are under
`stack-qualifications/0c27ab4fa0544a788fada53c0c7c4f3e`. No native exhaustion
cutoff, C++ frame size or guard diagnostic position was calibrated.

### Next dependency boundary

Explicit destructor designation/invocation is complete for the supported
nonvirtual scalar-subobject body group, including implicit trivial targets.
The next coherent lifecycle group is **recursive base/member destruction plans
and lexical cleanup scheduling**, preserving the C++ local-before-subobject
order, return/goto cleanup edges, arrays and exception specifications. Current
first-divergence examples are:

- `tests/general/100-destructor-explicit-return-subobjects.t` and
  `300-implicit-destructor-exception-specification.t`: class subobject
  destruction plans.
- `tests/general/200-destructor-body-local-before-base-destruction.t`: base
  subobject destructor plans.
- `tests/controls/100-substatement-declaration-destroyed.t`,
  `200-goto-lifetime-cleanup.t` and
  `tests/general/300-constructor-array-loop-enclosing-cleanup.t`: class storage
  destruction lowering.

Destructor template-ids, virtual entries, class prvalue/materialized receivers
and nonelided copies remain separate dependent capabilities. The broader
constructor/value/aggregate, dynamic-global/static/TLS, hidden-friend/ADL,
declaration and packed split-bit-field groups also remain. The exact residual
case/diagnostic clusters are in `remaining-clusters.json`; this handoff neither
declares full-stage completion nor advances the last audit.
